she crystallizes as Demi-Lich in a phylactery\n    13→- [[Tygras]] makes a deal with an arch-fey from [[Vecna]]'s plane; creates a weapon to bridge worlds\n    14→- [[Ranar Surefoot]] steals the [[Diadem of Dawn]] during the weapon's use; the weapon backfires\n    15→- [[Tygras]]'s superweapon destroys [[uma]], creating the desert; [[Tygras]] sealed in [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n    16→- The [[Matrim Pact]] formed: human sailors, gnomes of [[Fae'ish]], and [[Qual'Xar]] half-elves\n    17→- Covenants of Magic established after [[Tygras]]'s destruction — no being except the Alabaster Seat may possess such power again\n    18→- [[Kolyma]] (Last Curator) establishes Zodiac guardianship of the Zeniths\n    19→- The [[forgesworn]] order established; [[Thorin the Bronze|Thorin Bronzebeard]] builds the original Warforged army\n    20→\n    21→## Recent Past (Decades Before Campaign)\n    22→\n    23→- ~26 years ago: [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] born; parents make a deal with [[Reynardine]] for the philosopher's stone\n    24→- ~8 years ago: Nyx's dominant soul emerges; her sister taken; parents spurn Nyx\n    25→- ~7 years ago: [[Xyn]] born — the \"perfect vessel\" for the arch-fey's plan\n    26→- [[Mordred]] possesses [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle Hollingsworth]] (Gnome, NOT Goblin) at [[Forest's Zenith]]\n    27→- [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] founded as a soul-harvesting front\n    28→- [[Thorkon Fireforge]] betrays [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]] at [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]]'s urging\n    29→- [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] betrays [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]], hunts all [[forgesworn]]\n    30→- [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]] and [[Hlin Blunderbeat|Hlin]] flee [[Vag'Mahar]]; [[Hlin Blunderbeat|Hlin]] dies giving birth to [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]] and an older brother\n    31→- [[Blood Star]] kills [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]]\n    32→- [[Oblivion Wayne]] deposes her brother, unites all Drow under vampire rule\n    33→- [[Ebon Wayne]] captures [[Zodiac|Bat Zodiac]], discovers [[Spell Plague]]\n    34→- [[Vecna]] raises [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis Geth]] as Illithilich\n    35→- [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] corrupts the Elder Brain\n    36→- [[Ned'Zailok]] attacks [[Tower Bridge]]; [[Varis Liadon|Varis]]'s father [[Yeldan Liadon|Yeldan]] goes missing\n    37→- [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] covers the evacuation; [[Varis Liadon|Varis]] enlists\n    38→- [[Ned'Zailok]] defeated after 18 months of fighting\n    39→\n    40→## Campaign Era (136 Chapters)\n    41→\n    42→### Act 1 (Chapters 0-13) — The Awakening\n    43→\n    44→| Ch. | Event |\n    45→|-----|-------|\n    46→| 0 | Party recruited into [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]. [[Jorgen]] arrives pursued by kobolds. Sewer descent. |\n    47→| 1 | [[Dallor Dallorbill|Dallor]] pickpockets note from [[Jorgen]] — proof of setup. |\n    48→| 2 | [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle]] revealed as [[Mordred]]. \"2,000 souls.\" [[Jorgen]] killed, becomes revenant. [[Sabine Ra]] freed from phylactery, sacrifices herself. Crystalline hearts deposited in party. |\n    49→| 3-5 | [[Artificer's Guild]] rescue. [[Xerxes]] exposition. Soul tether. |\n    50→| 6 | Dopplegangers. [[V-Mardov]] rift opens. [[Varis Liadon|Varris]] arrives from alternate reality. |\n    51→| 7-8 | [[Jorgen]] revenant pursues [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle]]. [[Thornberry Shade]] ship. [[jafar]]/Sally intrigue. |\n    52→| 9 | First Prophecy ([[Ruth]]). |\n    53→| 11 | [[Golden Reach]] demons (Danelle/Markus) exposed at Festival of Light. [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart]]'s divine sense. |\n    54→| 12 | Second Prophecy (Hags). Pestilence discovered — brewed from children's souls. |\n    55→| 13 | Third Prophecy ([[Mia]]). [[Mia]] reveals she wiped party's memories. |\n    56→\n    57→### Act 2 (Chapters 14-40) — The Voyage\n    58→\n    59→| Ch. | Event |\n    60→|-----|-------|\n    61→| 17 | [[Nathan Yanagan]] (spy for [[jafar]]) aboard [[Thornberry Shade]]. Tiefling prejudice arc. |\n    62→| 19 | Soul glamours for [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]]. |\n    63→| 23 | Zodiac spirits revealed — they bet their immortality on the champions. |\n    64→| 24-25 | Tomb of the Last Curator. Four Zenith hieroglyphics. |\n    65→| 26 | [[Kelik Waystar]] revealed as air genasi prince. |\n    66→| 29 | Green Tides of St. Ashbury warning. |\n    67→| 30-31 | [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] revealed. [[Nocturnus Wayne]] kills cousin Dusken, becomes Drider King. |\n    69→| 36 | [[Fen Darkeyes|Devin Darkeyes]] found enslaved by the Aboleth. |\n    70→| 37-38 | Dvinter Castle / Phoenix arc. [[forgesworn]] connections revealed. |\n    71→\n    72→### Act 3 (Chapters 41-86) — The Escalation\n    73→\n    74→| Ch. | Event |\n    75→|-----|-------|\n    76→| 42+ | Campaign splits into parallel tracks. |\n    77→| ~66 | [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]]'s Homunculus Thraalm killed. |\n    78→| ~82 | Attempted revival of [[Zodiac|Falak]] the serpent using captured Mahen. |\n    79→| 86 | Reunion. Battle of the Distillery. |\n    80→\n    81→### Act 4 (Chapters 87-136) — The Endgame\n    82→\n    83→| Ch. | Event |\n    84→|-----|-------|\n    85→| 103 | Second Reunion. TPK and revival via myconid network. [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] carrying Kelemtor. |\n    86→| 104-105 | [[Reginald Providence]] killed (kenku in disguise). [[Anchor of War]] silenced. [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]] reveals complex motivations — wanted to save the party. |\n    88→| 107 | Ragnarok. Warforged awakening. [[Irethia]] revealed as aasimar created in image of [[Ashara Everstar]]. |\n    89→| 108 | Three races of elves' gods revealed. [[lolth]]'s \"Two Truths and a Lie.\" |\n    90→| 109 | [[Josh Coolbreeze]] cleanses [[Nocturnus Wayne|Nocturnus]] of vampirism. [[lolth]]-[[Raven Queen]] deal revealed. [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] disappears. |\n    91→| 113 | [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] framed for Pestilence \"oil spill.\" [[rimefang]]'s last ride. |\n    92→| 116 | Convergence and Betrayal — all planes converging into the Feywild. |\n    93→| 119 | Time paradox — [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] finds own dead body from 2 weeks ago, kickstarts his own heart. |\n    94→| 133 | [[Reynaldine]]'s true plan revealed: orchestrated everything to ascend to godhood and save Gabriel. Hearthstone exposed as triple agent. |\n    95→| 135 | Book of Shadows plot to open Harvest and Winter Zenith. [[Tiamat]] at Harvest Zenith. |\n    96→| 136 | [[Asmodeus]] killed by [[Josh Coolbreeze]]. Three specters: one into [[Nocturnus Wayne|Nocturnus]]. [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] becomes deity of Mardov's Feywild via Stone Table sacrifice. |\n    97→\n    98→## Prophesied: Gard'Daemon\n    99→\n   100→All four Zeniths held simultaneously. [[V-Mardov|V-MORDRED]]'s invasion repelled. The final configuration of Controllers, Champions, and Anchor Wielders activates.\n   101→\n   102→\n   103→## The True History Of Mardov (Extracted from PPTX)\n   104→\n   105→### Slide 1\n   106→\n   107→The True History Of\n   108→\n   109→---\n   110→\n   111→### Slide 2\n   112→\n   113→Year 0 - The Rose of Creation\n   114→\n   115→The Rose of Creation was the solution to the ever present conflict between Gods of Destruction and Gods of Creation. It is the culmination of countless Gods and Goddesses’ life's work, and is thusly an irresistible target for Destruction. One such petal on the Rose belonged to The Raven Queen of Fate who made a Pact with Vecna to twin their worlds to their mutual benefit.\n   116→\n   117→---\n   118→\n   119→### Slide 3\n   120→\n   121→The Death Pact\n   122→\n   123→The shared domain of Death would serve as the cornerstone of their agreement. This Pact is largely a mystery, however their specialties point to some grand revelation that will occur at the Fated time of the Raven Queen’s transition from Life to Death. This harvest of the limitless potential of the regent Goddess would culminate in a shared Secret with Vecna, God of Death and Secrets. The end result is a mutually beneficial understanding of What is Beyond the Death of Gods? Each believing they would best the other at the moment of the revelation as they were better equipped to take the advantage, the Pact was sealed.\n   124→\n   125→Mardov and Vodram, twinned worlds, were formed and with them 4 Zeniths capable of wielding the power of the domain of Death.\n   126→\n   127→---\n   128→\n   129→### Slide 4\n   130→\n   131→Zeniths\n   132→\n   133→From the Mountains to the Forest, in Winter or in Harvest, the Zeniths of power await their Anchor and Heart.\n   134→\n   135→There will be War and Death, Pestilence and Famine, but to Anchor the end of Guard’ Daemon, the battlefield will be tallied, its victor declared.\n   136→\n   137→For they wield a secret from beyond the grave of gods. Even in the fires of the Forge or the shadows of the Wilde, the light of Faith and the hope of Progenitors shall strike their Fated Heart.\n   138→\n   139→---\n   140→\n   141→### Slide 5\n   142→\n   143→Bringer of Oblivion\n   144→\n   145→Asmodeus, Bringer of Oblivion only took 100 years to find Mardov, and 12 months to signal the first Guard’Daemon. However, to truely seal Asmodeus away, Kolyma, the First and Last Curator of Mardov had to sacrifice himself to activate the Zeniths and turn them against Oblivion. Unable to fully destroy the God, Asmodeus was sealed in thirteen amulets, to be scattered to the guardians on the edges of existence.\n   146→\n   147→---\n   148→\n   149→### Slide 6\n   150→\n   151→Inheritors of the Mantle\n   152→\n   153→In the Death of Kolyma, the potential he captured to answer the Curator’s Plea was bestowed in four mantles.\n   154→\n   155→The Alabaster Mantle of Arcane Magic\n   156→\n   157→The Ivy Mantle of Natural Magic\n   158→\n   159→The Palladium Mantle of Divine Magic\n   160→\n   161→The Crystalline Mantle of Mortal Magic\n   162→"}]},"uuid":"99e75f4c-ab07-4a1c-8252-8adde15814f0","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:57.397Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ec3fa353-6ff6-40e3-a301-dd0443b85569","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"35de766c-67e6-49a0-9a30-59842e820747","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a1afca0d6ba2fa3bf","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NZJdsHqQQV1ZKM3Sntpt56","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Story Arcs & Narrative Structure\"\n     4→---\n     5→\n     6→# Story Arcs & Narrative Structure\n     7→\n     8→## The Overarching Story\n     9→\n    10→The campaign revolves around [[Mordred|Ramses/Mordred]], whose plan unfolds in three stages:\n    11→\n    12→1. **Gets the Avatar of the [[Raven Queen]] to sacrifice her immortality** -- this creates a rift between [[Mardov]] and [[V-Mardov]] where [[Vecna]]'s Avatar rules\n    13→2. **Collects [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]]'s gifts to control her power** -- originally wanted to resurrect [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]], his lost love, but after her spirit shattered he pivoted to controlling her fragments. Gave up his phylactery to take out the [[Artificer's Guild]], gaining [[Xerxes]]'s body (corrupting the individual beings that make up [[Xerxes]])\n    14→3. **Destroys all the keystones** -- with his newfound body, [[Mordred]] has one goal: bring about the invasion of this realm by V-MORDRED by destroying all the keystones\n    15→\n    16→### The Keystones\n    17→\n    18→| Name | Gemstone | Location | Conflict Type | Horseman/Anchor |\n    19→|------|----------|----------|---------------|-----------------|\n    20→| [[Heart of the Wilde]] | Emerald | [[Forest's Zenith]] | Dungeon | Death |\n    21→| [[Heart of the Forge]] | Garnet | [[Mountain's Zenith]] | Dwarven underdark conflict | War |\n    22→| [[Heart of the Faith]] | Sapphire | [[Winter's Zenith]] | Winter's wasteland | Pestilence |\n    23→| [[Heart of the Progenitors]] | Ruby | [[Harvest's Zenith]] | Cataclysmic, philosopher's stone | Famine |\n    24→\n    25→---\n    26→\n    27→## Act I — The Awakening (Chapters 0-13)\n    28→\n    29→The party is recruited into the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]. They encounter [[Mordred]] possessing [[Finkle Hollingsworth]], [[Sabine Ra]] is freed from her phylactery, and crystalline heart fragments are deposited in the party. Three prophecies are received from [[Ruth]], the Hags, and [[Mia]], establishing the four-zenith framework.\n    30→\n    31→**Key beats:**\n    32→- Party death and resurrection by [[Sabine Ra]] (Chapter 2)\n    33→- [[V-Mardov]] rift opens; [[Varis Liadon|Varris]] arrives from the alternate reality (Chapter 6)\n    34→- First, Second, and Third Prophecies received (Chapters 9, 12, 13)\n    35→- [[Mia]] reveals she wiped the party's memories\n    36→\n    37→---\n    38→\n    39→## Act II — The Voyage (Chapters 14-40)\n    40→\n    41→The party sails aboard the [[Thornberry Shade]], encounters the zodiac spirits (who bet their immortality on the champions), explores the [[Tomb of the Last Curator]], and splits into parallel tracks.\n    42→\n    43→**Key beats:**\n    44→- Tiefling prejudice arc; soul glamours for [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] (Chapters 17-19)\n    45→- Zodiac spirits revealed; they bet their immortality on the champions (Chapter 23)\n    46→- Tomb of the Last Curator; four zenith hieroglyphics decoded (Chapters 24-25)\n    47→- [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] revealed; [[Reynaldine]]'s network exposed (Chapters 30-31)\n    48→- [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]]'s letter found inside [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]]'s axe; [[forgesworn]] lore revealed (Chapter 34)\n    49→\n    50→---\n    51→\n    52→## Act III — The Escalation (Chapters 41-86)\n    53→\n    54→Campaign splits into parallel tracks. Individual character arcs deepen.\n    55→\n    56→### The Cloud City Arc ([[windhaven]])\n    57→\n    58→The party arrives in [[windhaven]] via a portal from the [[Kraken of the Sand Seas]]. Key events:\n    59→\n    60→1. **[[Tiberius D. Rex]]** receives a premonition of the party's arrival\n    61→2. **Research at the Windmill Willow**: [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] researches the zodiacs (astral chart showing the 12 animals, one per month); [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] researches the [[Diadem of Dawn]]; [[Randal]] researches the gods\n    62→3. **[[Leonidas of Summer's Last Light]]** puts the party on trial in his sphinx demi-plane:\n    63→   - [[Varis Liadon|Varys]] requests help against [[Vecna]], is granted a vision of [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]] grabbing the [[Heart of the Forge]] and jumping into lava\n    64→   - [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] requests help to save [[Xyn]], meets [[Juliannia of the Summer Court]] -- her real patron\n    65→   - [[Fen Darkeyes|Fen]] wishes he and his brother never fell under the Aboleth's influence; wish granted\n    66→   - [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]] alone is deemed unworthy (meaning unknown)\n    67→   - [[Tiberius D. Rex|Tiberious]] has undergone this process 75 times, making him far older than the 76 he appears\n    68→4. **The Cabal of Horns**: [[Randal]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] discover an underground tiefling society led by [[Winnipeg Redvine]], uncovering a child-kidnapping plot by Lady [[Mathia Nuum]]\n    69→5. **Reynaldine's Demand**: The party encounters [[Reynaldine|Reynaldine of the Autumn Court]] collecting Elemental Seals. [[Kelik Temptus]] dies when absorbed into his amulet. [[Reynaldine]] already has Fire and Earth seals; tasks the party with retrieving the Water amulet from [[Lapis Poseidon]]\n    70→6. **[[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]]'s Stag Form**: Discovers her stag form resembles [[Shi-Shi-Gami|Shi Shi Gami]], with antlers forming the [[Zodiac Locket]]\n    71→7. **Cloud City Falling**: The party fights to save Cloud City, encounters [[Zepheeron the Bronze]], battles at Jackfall Landing, and is rescued by [[Captain Liza]] on the [[Thornberry Wilde]]\n    72→8. **[[Reynaldine]]'s True Nature Revealed**: [[Randal]] uses Legend Lore and discovers [[Reynaldine]] is actually 4 beings: [[Juliannia of the Summer Court]], [[Graz'zt|Grazz't the Demon Prince]], [[Gabriel the Kind]] (Archangel), and [[Reynaldine|Reynardine, Prince of Harvest]]\n    73→9. **[[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] meets [[Xyn]]**: Via Lorelai's magical broach, the twin sisters finally communicate. [[Xyn]] reveals [[Gabriel the Kind]] is her patron (lives in teddy bear \"Mr. Happy\"), that the [[Mountain's Zenith]] opening released [[Barachiel the Righteous]], and that [[Euclid]]'s book *The Origin of Genasi and the Rebirth of the Progenitors* is the key to the [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n    74→10. **The Harvest's Zenith spotted**: [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]] touches the satellite and sees a hanging upside-down city with the [[Harvest's Zenith]] -- a dual pyramid with 7 floors all rotating out of phase\n    75→\n    76→---\n    77→\n    78→## Act IV — The Endgame (Chapters 87-136)\n    79→\n    80→The threads converge. Character arcs resolve. Gods die. The world changes.\n    81→\n    82→**Key beats:**\n    83→- Second Reunion; TPK and revival via myconid network (Chapter 103)\n    84→- [[Reginald Providence]] killed; [[Anchor of War]] silenced; Therzadun thwarted (Chapters 104-105)\n    85→- Declaration of War on gods; [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]] reveals complex motivations (Chapter 106)\n    86→- Ragnarok; Warforged awakening; [[Irethia]] revealed as aasimar (Chapter 107)\n    87→- [[lolth]]'s \"Two Truths and a Lie\" (Chapter 108)\n    88→- [[Asmodeus]] killed by [[Josh Coolbreeze]]; three specters escape, one enters [[Nocturnus Wayne]] (Chapter 136)\n    89→- [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] becomes deity of [[Mardov]]'s [[Feywild]] via Stone Table sacrifice (Chapter 136)\n    90→\n    91→---\n    92→\n    93→## Global Conflicts\n    94→\n    95→### The Orc-Human War ([[DawnHaven]])\n    96→\n    97→**Location:** [[dawnpass]]/[[windhaven]]/[[Eavallan]] vs [[Stra'Franar]]/[[Fae'ish]]/[[Tygras Logath]]\n    98→\n    99→**Reveal:** At the [[Magister's Circle]], an envoy from [[dawnpass]] (notably containing zero Half-Orcs -- there have been none in the entire game) requests aid against an army of sorcerers wielding draconic-blood-fueled destruction similar to the Tiefling army that destroyed [[uma]].\n   100→\n   101→**Root cause:** The Orc Chieftain and former Gladiator Pit Champion ([[Logath HellEye|Logath]]) made a deal with the same fae demon as [[Tygras]], causing intense bloodlust and madness. This makes him a target for [[Mordred]], who uses Logath's influence to cut off food from the fertile Harvestlands north of [[windhaven]].\n   102→\n   103→**Connection to the Zenith:** This is the location of the [[Anchor of Famine]], tied to the [[Harvest's Zenith]]. The resurgence of Orc sorcerers matches exactly with when it last rained. The [[Matrim Pact]] was between human sailors of old [[uma]], gnomes of [[Fae'ish]], and [[Qual'Xar]] half-elves -- they promised sanctuary atop the [[Maturin Turtle]].\n   104→\n   105→**The Hidden City:** The city is actually a buried pyramid, the great ziggurat housing the [[Harvest's Zenith]]. Inside, [[Tygras]] himself (or the arcane being he became after his failed ascension) has bid his time feeding on internal zenith energies. Destroying his echo rips a hole in the Fae and reveals the fae demon who gave [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] her powers.\n   106→\n   107→**Nyx's Choice:** The demon was the one who made the pact with [[Tygras]], and [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] was his heir. By destroying the last echo of [[Tygras]], future tiefling children can be prevented. [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] inherits the mark of [[Tygras]] (actually Sable's Beacon, an ancient fae marking). She can either rid the world of \"demon children\" or rebuild the lost empire by adding tieflings to the [[Matrim Pact]] and reviving the guardian turtle.\n   108→\n   109→**Reviving the Guardian:** Requires (1) removing the [[Anchor of Famine]] from the top of the zenith (guarded by the Harvest Effigy), (2) returning the [[Heart of the Progenitors]] to the crevasse in the middle of [[Tygras Logath]], (3) speaking the words: *\"On his back all vows are made, a haven for the continent of dawn paid.\"* Upon finishing, the turtle skeleton below the bay is revived -- a massive turtle carrying an Atlantean city, serving as THE landing place between [[Mardov]] and the [[Feywild]].\n   110→\n   111→---\n   112→\n   113→## Character Arc Themes\n   114→\n   115→### [[Sprocket Overspark]] — The Pursuit of Fun\n   116→\n   117→**Question:** Is it possible for responsibility to be fun?\n   118→\n   119→[[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]]'s fate as the next Avatar of the [[Raven Queen]] cannot be downplayed. No matter how he tries, this is his overarching conflict: *\"You cannot run from your fate.\"* To learn this is to understand what it means to become the next [[Raven Queen]].\n   120→\n   121→His rise of infamy: Trixie -> [[Thornberry Shade]] -> [[Qual'Xar]] -> [[Magister's Circle]] & [[Conquis Abbey]] -> Entire Material Plane. The deeds he deems \"fun\" provide notoriety, making it harder to be underestimated.\n   122→\n   123→### [[Brigit Wildfire]] — Self-Discovery\n   124→\n   125→**Question:** What makes me special?\n   126→\n   127→[[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] is a [[Progenitors|Progenitor]] -- one of the original beings created in Eden. This grants her the ability to open the [[Harvest's Zenith]] without destroying the elemental seals. There is no reason for this other than fate -- the Progenitors were fated to return to help stop [[Gard'Daemon]].\n   128→\n   129→### [[Randal]] — Apotheosis and Pacifism\n   130→\n   131→**Question:** What does it take to become a god?\n   132→\n   133→[[Randal]]'s decision to become a pacifist is valiant but ultimately flawed, for he claimed victory while he still has goals outstanding. His ideal: *\"Victory is only satisfying if achieved without violence.\"*\n   134→\n   135→Foils to his pacifism:\n   136→- *\"Violence is Victory\"* -- [[Logath HellEye|Logath]], Orcish Leader\n   137→- *\"Peace is what I will achieve after all my enemies have been defeated by my hand\"* -- [[Evelyandrin of the Spring Court]]\n   138→- *\"Some things are worth fighting for\"* -- [[Reynaldine]]\n   139→- *\"Nothing is worth fighting for, I will endure and adapt\"* -- [[Grumblesin of the Winter Court]]\n   140→\n   141→---\n   142→\n   143→## Player Background Tie-Ins\n   144→\n   145→### [[Sprocket Overspark]]\n   146→- Has a yurt outside [[hinderstap]] village in [[monethern]]\n   147→- Sent by [[lillith]], the Platinum Seat of the Diviners Covenant, to investigate a village where inhabitants are normal by day but ravenous and murderous by night\n   148→- Being killed in the village forces the murdered to stay permanently, reborn each morning (happened to Giddean Clary, Mustard Bard, with his Basset Hound Darling)\n   149→- A portal/wound links [[hinderstap]] to the [[shadowfell]]\n   150→\n   151→### [[Brigit Wildfire]]\n   152→- Criminal contact: [[Firekeeper Morn Loregramar|Firekeeper Lorgrammar]], orc shaman whose people betrayed him and assaulted [[Eavallan]] Forest, [[Stra'Franar]], and Last Tower Bridge\n   153→- She is a [[Progenitors|Progenitor]], one of the original beings created in Eden\n   154→- [[Eavallan]] Forest is of the [[Feywild]], the earliest location where elves first came to [[Mardov]]\n   155→- The original sacking of [[Eavallan]] by the orcs set into motion the steps to open the [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n   156→- Progenitor blood was thought to be in [[uma]], but the bloodline was still strong; [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]]'s parents were drawn to [[Eavallan]] because it was closest to the Zenith\n   157→\n   158→---\n   159→\n   160→## Story Beats Still To Hit\n   161→\n   162→### [[Randal]] and Apotheosis\n   163→- The gods were the first to uncover universal truths\n   164→- Once a truth is uncovered, Apotheosis becomes possible (see [[lore/cosmology|Cosmology]])\n   165→- [[Reynaldine]]'s plan to collapse all planes is his miracle attempt\n   166→\n   167→### The Raven Queen's Transition\n   168→- The Death Pact between the [[Raven Queen]] and [[Vecna]] hinges on the Raven Queen's transition from Life to Death\n   169→- The harvest of the regent Goddess's limitless potential will culminate in a shared Secret with [[Vecna]]\n   170→- Both believe they will best the other at the moment of revelation\n   171→"}]},"uuid":"5e211ca0-911f-459a-b4b4-efcb87abcf49","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:58.012Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"35de766c-67e6-49a0-9a30-59842e820747","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

[LOW] = reconstructed from context.\n    12→\n    13→---\n    14→\n    15→## BRIGIT (Wild Spirit of Fire)\n    16→\n    17→**Player**: Liz\n    18→**Class**: Druid (Circle of the Moon) / Barbarian multiclass\n    19→**Race**: Changeling/Shapeshifter\n    20→**Key Items**: Anchor of Death (locket), Stag Zodiac\n    21→**Track**: Tieflings -> Distillery -> Good Guys Guild\n    22→\n    23→### Arc Summary\n    24→\n    25→Brigit begins as a chaotic, multi-formed druid struggling with the many voices in her head. She becomes a Progenitor through a ritual in Eden with the Children of the Forest, gains custody of the Anchor of Death in her locket, and carries the Stag Zodiac. Her arc is one of identity -- learning to channel her rage and multiplicity into purpose. She is the Wild Spirit of Fire, and through her shapeshifting, serves as the party's most versatile combatant and emotional anchor. By Act 4 she has ascended the locket, lost her elemental powers during the Harvest Zenith battle, and must find her way forward without her primary coping mechanism.\n    26→\n    27→### Timeline\n    28→\n    29→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    30→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    31→| 0-2 | Joins the party; party killed and resurrected by Sabine Ra | [HIGH] Establishes the campaign's death/rebirth theme |\n    32→| 5-7 | Hall of the Raven; early adventures | [MEDIUM] Party cohesion building |\n...\n</persisted-output>"}]},"uuid":"5562aae2-db34-496a-87a6-1e2f9e7e27d6","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:58.593Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"442ece31-3014-4411-9cf3-13750a739989","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"91e1fb7f-4307-453c-9a75-63288387bd62","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a1afca0d6ba2fa3bf","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016sm1vR847TAhJ3NMhZvmP3","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: creature\n     3→name: \"Reginald Providence, Lich of Therzadun Chained God of Madness\"\n     4→source: [\"D&D Beyond Homebrew\"]\n     5→ac: 17\n     6→hp: 231\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→# Reginald Providence, Lich of Therzadun Chained God of Madness\n    10→\n    11→**AC** 17 | **HP** 231\n    12→**Stats**: STR 11 | DEX 18 | CON 20 | INT 22 | WIS 14 | CHA 14\n    13→**Senses**: Truesight 120ft.\n    14→**Skills**: Arcana +14, Perception +7\n    15→**Condition Immunities**: Charmed, Exhaustion, Frightened, Paralyzed, Poisoned\n    16→\n    17→## Special Traits\n    18→\n    19→Far Realm Parasite. Inside the lich&rsquo;s torso dwells a wormlike parasite that contains the lich&rsquo;s soul. When the lich dies, it implodes into the parasite, which then vanishes into the Far Realm. In 2d4 days, the parasite causes the lich to reappear within 1d4 miles of where it died. If the lich died inside a magic circle cast to contain Undead, the lich instead reappears as an otyugh with all the lich&rsquo;s memories.\n    20→Legendary Resistance (4/Day). If the lich fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.\n    21→Unusual Nature. The lich doesn&rsquo;t need air, food, drink, or sleep.\n    22→\n    23→## Actions\n    24→\n    25→Multiattack. The lich makes one Parasitic Tentacle attack or uses Spellcasting. The lich also uses Psychic Whisper twice.\n    26→Parasitic Tentacle. Melee Weapon Attack: +9 to hit, reach 10 ft., one creature. Hit: 25 (6d6 + 4) piercing damage plus 25 (6d6 + 4) necrotic damage. The target must succeed on a DC 17 Constitution saving throw or be poisoned. The poisoned target can repeat the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. The third time the target fails the save, the target dies and dissolves into a gibbering mouther that obeys the lich and uses the target&rsquo;s initiative.\n    27→Psychic Whisper. The lich targets one creature it can see within 120 feet of itself. The target must succeed on a DC 17 Wisdom saving throw or take 25 (6d6 + 4) psychic damage and be stunned until the end of the lich&rsquo;s next turn as incomprehensible whispers fill the target&rsquo;s mind.\n    28→Spellcasting. The lich casts one of the following spells, using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 18):\n    29→Spell slot: 5 additional 5th level spells.\n    30→At will: detect magic, mage hand, prestidigitation, armor of Agathys, mage armor, mass suggestion, shadow of moil\n    31→2/day each: dispel magic, hunger of Hadar, lightning bolt\n    32→1/day each: arcane eye, dimension door, plane shift (self only), crown of stars (13 total motes), maddening darkness\n    33→\n    34→## Legendary Actions\n    35→\n    36→The lich gains the following legendary action options, which replace all of the lich&rsquo;s legendary actions.\n    37→Tentacles.&nbsp;The lich makes one attack with its tentacles.\n    38→Whispers of Madness.&nbsp;The lich uses mass suggestion which targets creatures currently in darkness. Creatures have +5 to the spell save if they have already passed a check against suggestion before.\n    39→Cast Spell (Costs 1&ndash;3 Actions).&nbsp;The lich uses a spell slot to cast a 1st-, 2nd-, or 3rd-level spell that it has prepared. Doing so costs 1 legendary action per level of the spell.\n    40→\n    41→"}]},"uuid":"d1484491-28be-413d-be5f-fa8fb569258f","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:04.506Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"91e1fb7f-4307-453c-9a75-63288387bd62","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"2c51dfd3-26dc-41a9-b8c0-8de6baea7822","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a1afca0d6ba2fa3bf","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TJffeojNMc6siaRpdU53mV","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: chapter\n     3→act: 5\n     4→number: 104\n     5→title: \"Chapter 104\"\n     6→pcs_present: []\n     7→locations: []\n     8→npcs_introduced: []\n     9→items_found: []\n    10→source: [\"NFS/Chapters\"]\n    11→has_recording: false\n    12→has_slideshow: false\n    13→adaptation_priority: \"\"\n    14→key_moments: []\n    15→visual_beats: []\n    16→dialogue_highlights: []\n    17→---\n    18→\n    19→## DM Lead-In\n    20→\n    21→### Slide 1\n    22→\n    23→Chapter 104\n    24→\n    25→---\n    26→\n    27→### Slide 2\n    28→\n    29→MAP\n    30→\n    31→“Well, well, well finally. I have all three of you here. Welcome, to the First Forge”\n    32→\n    33→~ Reginald Providence\n    34→\n    35→---\n    36→\n    37→### Slide 3\n    38→\n    39→Last We Saw Our Heroes…\n    40→\n    41→---\n    42→\n    43→### Slide 4\n    44→\n    45→Goals Cheat Sheet\n    46→\n    47→Main Goal - Prevent Guard’Daemon\n    48→\n    49→Mountain’s Zenith: Beneath Vag Mahar\u000bAnchor of War: Daina’s Axe (unascended)\u000bHeart of the Forge: Fused with Axe\u000bGod: Therzadun (Sealed behind Iron Doors)\n    50→\n    51→Forest’s Zenith: Above Machu\u000bAnchor of Death: Brigit’s Locket\u000bHeart of the Wilde: Fused with Finkle Hollingsworth/Vecna\u000bGod: Vecna (Avatar Awakened)\n    52→\n    53→Winter’s Zenith: Bynar / Halls of Winter\u000bAnchor of Pestilence: Horn of Winter?\u000bHeart of the Faith: Fen Darkeyes took it with him\u000bGod: Tiamat (Avatar Awakened)\n    54→\n    55→Harvest’s Zenith: Autumn Feywilde in ruins of old Uma\u000bAnchor of Famine: Diadem last seen in the Halls of Reflection\u000bHeart of the Progenitors: In the hands of Grazz’t Demon Prince of Pleasure\u000bGod: Asmodeus (Sealed with Grazz’t in possession of 10/13 seals, missing only 2 Angel Seals)\n    56→\n    57→---\n    58→\n    59→### Slide 5\n    60→\n    61→The Smaller Picture\n    62→\n    63→The Vag Mahar is occupied territory by the Mindflayers beholden to Therzadun. Their leader, “Ghost Eye” Thorin, nowhere to be found\n    64→\n    65→The party is currently trying to ascend the Anchor of War within the First Forge, however there was a trap, half the party now must fight Vellem the Reforged and the other half must fight Reginald Providence, Lich of Therzadun.\n    66→\n    67→The party arrived with the Craftkeeper Rebels who agreed to divert the majority of the cities defenses to a mine known to serve as a well for Drab\n    68→\n    69→---\n    70→\n    71→### Slide 6\n    72→\n    73→The Mountain is Coming!\n    74→\n    75→The party spent the first half of their day warning nearby cities and townships of the impending doom presented by Goldemdrum the Elemental Evil of Earth’s migration to the fated battlefield with Fire, Wind and Water. Unlike Wind and Fire, who are already there with minimal collateral damage, Golemdrum seems content to cause as much damage as possible on his way.\n    76→\n    77→---\n    78→\n    79→### Slide 7\n    80→\n    81→Hearing the Voices of Monethern\n    82→\n    83→One stop on their way was Monethern, the birthplace of their friend Fen Darkeyes, whom they had hoped to find while warning the city. Instead, they met with a number of the Voices of Veritasium Palace, the most sympathetic of which was Trevor the Voice of Heart. Together, they agreed to send help to the party’s assault on the Vag Mahar and evacuate everyone they could!\n    84→\n    85→Trevor Cordin - Heart\n    86→\n    87→Coralinus Tovensmore - Truth\n    88→\n    89→Lumeire Huntsman - Paupers\n    90→\n    91→---\n    92→\n    93→### Slide 8\n    94→\n    95→Beaten, Bloody & Nearly Alone\n    96→\n    97→Bergs “Barnabus” Fireforge survived his parties delve into the White Hills of St. Brutus purely on a technicality. His mushroom reanimated body firmly planting itself into the ground with every step, he made his way to the Vag Mahar after being drawn to the hundreds of voices of the Drab within. He carried with him the only other surviving member of his party Kelemtor, though merely the risen’s head remained. Through, luck or fate, he was reunited with his sister Daina and her friends just in time to meet Theosodia and the Craftkeepers for his third attempt at freeing the Dwarven Capital.\n    98→\n    99→His every colored beard now bleached white, unable to control the flow of myconid information from Birtcoin. Our Craftmaster is in a bad way.\n   100→\n   101→---\n   102→\n   103→### Slide 9\n   104→\n   105→Beefed up Main Gate\n   106→\n   107→Repaired/Reinforced Upper Tier Gate\n   108→\n   109→Trashed Docks\n   110→\n   111→Crowded with Drab\n   112→\n   113→Broken Timekeeping Device & old tunnel\n   114→\n   115→Collapsed Tunnel Leading Up\n   116→\n   117→The Military Strategy\n   118→\n   119→The Craftkeepers head to the Blackbeard Lower Ward to the MindFlayer Mine to create a distraction and see if assaulting the Mindflayer hive beneath.\n   120→\n   121→The Party would sneak through the Blackbeard’s Middle Ward to Upper Ward and over to the Stone Sanctum in the Brownbeard’s Upper Ward, passing through the Redbeard’s Upper Ward on the way.\n   122→\n   123→---\n   124→\n   125→### Slide 10\n   126→\n   127→The Troubling Politics of Drow\n   128→\n   129→While sneaking their way through the city, it became clear that there was a tiered system for citizens with Drow in a quasi-foreign dignitary status. Once at the Blackbeard’s Upper Ward, aka Elysium, Nocturnas Wayne was approached by Veyrea Hysteil, leader of a faction of Drow who believe that the Darkest Night prophecy is still possible. The party avoids a fight by declaring fealty to the Wayne Family. They agree to meet later, if there is one.\n   130→\n   131→Note that pre-vamipire/Shadar Kai Nocturnus has a wanted poster up. All Drow except 3 are now Vampires, meaning one of the 3 must be the one to set the Darkest Night.\n   132→\n   133→---\n   134→\n   135→### Slide 11\n   136→\n   137→Encounter with Plaguebringer\n   138→\n   139→At the ruins of the Dragon’s Carapace Workshop, the party encounters Pyraxis Phyrexia, Vampiric Illithid Lich of Vecna and creator of the Drab Spellplague, who gives them a warning about the two-eyed Beholder Vellem the Reforged, so that they can be better equipped to defeat his rival, Reginald Providence. The runin was tense, however the disgraced Mindflayer left before things could get too violent.\n   140→\n   141→His warning was that if both eyes look upon you, you will be transported to join Reginald within the Stone Sanctum, which would be a trap.\n   142→\n   143→---\n   144→\n   145→### Slide 12\n   146→\n   147→Traps Triggered, Let’s Split Up Gang!\n   148→\n   149→Inside the Stone Sanctum:\n   150→\n   151→Daina “Diesa” Fireforge\n   152→\n   153→Bergs “Barnabus” Fireforge\n   154→\n   155→Nocturnas Wayne\n   156→\n   157→Reginald Providence\n   158→\n   159→a mysterious huddled henchman\n   160→\n   161→Outside the Stone Sanctum:\n   162→\n   163→Varis Laidon\n   164→\n   165→Sprocket Overspark\n   166→\n   167→Brigit Wildfire\n   168→\n   169→HAL\n   170→\n   171→Vellem The Reforged\n   172→\n   173→\n   174→## Forgesworn Maghar\n   175→\n   176→### Slide 1\n   177→\n   178→Forgesworn Maghar\n   179→\n   180→---\n   181→\n   182→### Slide 2\n   183→\n   184→Forgesworn Maghar\n   185→\n   186→Moradin’s Coat of Arms:\n   187→\n   188→Each has a transferable tattoo passed from Maghar -> Apprentice\n   189→\n   190→Gifted to the Dwarves thanks to Maghar Greyspear, Paragon of Muradin\n   191→\n   192→Combined effort by Laudgar Duergar to create Warforged out of all 13 Maghar to fight Mindflayers\n   193→\n   194→13 of which must have died to open the Mountain’s Zenith\n   195→\n   196→Bronzebeards Creed\n   197→\n   198→\"In the thrill of creation, there is one dwarven kin,\n   199→\n   200→I shall guard the secrets of my art as closely as I study them\n   201→\n   202→Upon Muradin's Honor, we shall guard the realm with his gift\n   203→\n   204→The First Forge bind them, Laudgar's price we gladly give\"\n   205→\n   206→---\n   207→\n   208→### Slide 3\n   209→\n   210→Meet The Maghar: Helm\n   211→\n   212→Bless Artifact: Helm.\n   213→\n   214→Jakob Hardhammer blesses the helm of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed helm grants the wearer +2 to spell saves while within range. Only one Helm may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   215→\n   216→---\n   217→\n   218→### Slide 4\n   219→\n   220→Meet the Maghar: Breastplate\n   221→\n   222→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Breastplate.\n   223→\n   224→Bartrim Hadrin blesses the Breastplate of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Breastplate grants the wearer +2 to AC while within range. Only one Breastplate may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   225→\n   226→---\n   227→\n   228→### Slide 5\n   229→\n   230→Meet the Maghar: Pauldrons\n   231→\n   232→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Pauldrons.\n   233→\n   234→Gareth Gorunn blesses the Boots of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Pauldrons grants the wearer the ability to lift twice their normal amount and advantage on Strength Checks while within range. Only one Set of Pauldrons may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   235→\n   236→---\n   237→\n   238→### Slide 6\n   239→\n   240→Meet the Maghar: Gauntlets\n   241→\n   242→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Gauntlets.\n   243→\n   244→Gavin Haldrin blesses the Gauntlets of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Gauntlets grants the wearer +2 to hit on attacks from within range. Only one Gauntlets may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   245→\n   246→---\n   247→\n   248→### Slide 7\n   249→\n   250→Meet the Maghar: Greaves\n   251→\n   252→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Greaves.\n   253→\n   254→Hammond Jack blesses the Greaves of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Greaves grants the wearer a swim speed within magma and immunity to fire damage while within range. Only one Pair of Greaves may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   255→\n   256→---\n   257→\n   258→### Slide 8\n   259→\n   260→Meet the Maghar: Boots\n   261→\n   262→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Boots.\n   263→\n   264→Bardin Caveskin blesses the Boots of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Boots grants the wearer +10ft of movement while within range. Only one Pair of Boots may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   265→\n   266→---\n   267→\n   268→### Slide 9\n   269→\n   270→Meet the Maghar: Clockwork\n   271→\n   272→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Clockwork.\n   273→\n   274→Decan Smithrunner blesses the Belt of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Belt grants the wearer +2/-2 to any roll once per turn while within range. Only one Clockwork may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   275→\n   276→---\n   277→\n   278→### Slide 10\n   279→\n   280→Meet the Maghar: Belt\n   281→\n   282→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Belt.\n   283→\n   284→Reginald Providence blesses the Belt of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Belt grants the wearer the ability to hold two sets of concentration while within range. By passing ownership of a spell currently being concentrated on while touching the statue. Only one Belt may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   285→\n   286→---\n   287→\n   288→### Slide 11\n   289→\n   290→Meet the Maghar: Sword\n   291→\n   292→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Sword.\n   293→\n   294→Slyvia Hadrin blesses the Sword of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Sword grants the wearer +2 to damage on attacks from within range. Only one Sword may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   295→\n   296→---\n   297→\n   298→### Slide 12\n   299→\n   300→Meet the Maghar: Shield\n   301→\n   302→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Shield.\n   303→\n   304→Pickle Rockgut blesses the Shield of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Shield grants the wearer +2 AC while within range. Only one Shield may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   305→\n   306→---\n   307→\n   308→### Slide 13\n   309→\n   310→Meet the Maghar: Axe\n   311→\n   312→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Axe.\n   313→\n   314→Throkon Fireforge blesses the Axe of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Axe grants the wearer +2 to damage on attacks from within range. Only one Axe may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   315→\n   316→---\n   317→\n   318→### Slide 14\n   319→\n   320→Meet the Maghar: Hammer\n   321→\n   322→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Hammer.\n   323→\n   324→Hiln Blunderbeat blesses the Hammer of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Hammer grants the wearer +2 to damage on attacks from within range. Only one Hammer may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   325→\n   326→---\n   327→\n   328→### Slide 15\n   329→\n   330→Meet the Maghar: Spear\n   331→\n   332→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Spear.\n   333→\n   334→Torvald Greyspear blesses the Spear of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Spear grants the wearer double the effects of all other Channel Divinities targeting the wielder. Only one Spear may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   335→\n   336→---\n   337→\n   338→### Slide 16\n   339→"}]},"uuid":"1182f866-da23-4338-a687-b4a63d4653e0","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:06.840Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2c51dfd3-26dc-41a9-b8c0-8de6baea7822","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"6157fd32-ec3f-4586-84ca-a15a7bda134f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a1afca0d6ba2fa3bf","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01G5RfxMV5iKqWABEu6m3Moq","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: chapter\n     3→act: 5\n     4→number: 105\n     5→title: \"Chapter 105\"\n     6→pcs_present: []\n     7→locations: []\n     8→npcs_introduced: []\n     9→items_found: []\n    10→source: [\"NFS/Chapters\"]\n    11→has_recording: false\n    12→has_slideshow: false\n    13→adaptation_priority: \"\"\n    14→key_moments: []\n    15→visual_beats: []\n    16→dialogue_highlights: []\n    17→---\n    18→\n    19→## DM Lead-In\n    20→\n    21→### Slide 1\n    22→\n    23→Chapter 105\n    24→\n    25→---\n    26→\n    27→### Slide 2\n    28→\n    29→MAP\n    30→\n    31→As the fire bellows on the forgotten forge, and the blood star is found\n    32→\n    33→The Anchor of War shall be silenced, as the new paragon is crowned.\n    34→\n    35→~ Mia, Third Prophecy of the Argosian Woods Witches\n    36→\n    37→---\n    38→\n    39→### Slide 3\n    40→\n    41→Last We Saw Our Heroes…\n    42→\n    43→---\n    44→\n    45→### Slide 4\n    46→\n    47→Goals Cheat Sheet\n    48→\n    49→Main Goal - Prevent Guard’Daemon\n    50→\n    51→Mountain’s Zenith: Beneath Vag Mahar\u000bAnchor of War: Daina’s Axe (unascended)\u000bHeart of the Forge: Fused with Axe\u000bGod: Therzadun (Sealed behind Iron Doors)\n    52→\n    53→Forest’s Zenith: Above Machu\u000bAnchor of Death: Brigit’s Locket\u000bHeart of the Wilde: Fused with Finkle Hollingsworth/Vecna\u000bGod: Vecna (Avatar Awakened)\n    54→\n    55→Winter’s Zenith: Bynar / Halls of Winter\u000bAnchor of Pestilence: Horn of Winter?\u000bHeart of the Faith: Fen Darkeyes took it with him\u000bGod: Tiamat (Avatar Awakened)\n    56→\n    57→Harvest’s Zenith: Autumn Feywilde in ruins of old Uma\u000bAnchor of Famine: Diadem last seen in the Halls of Reflection\u000bHeart of the Progenitors: In the hands of Grazz’t Demon Prince of Pleasure\u000bGod: Asmodeus (Sealed with Grazz’t in possession of 10/13 seals, missing only 2 Angel Seals)\n    58→\n    59→---\n    60→\n    61→### Slide 5\n    62→\n    63→The Smaller Picture\n    64→\n    65→The Vag Mahar is occupied territory by the Mindflayers beholden to Therzadun. Their leader, “Ghost Eye” Thorin, nowhere to be found\n    66→\n    67→The party is currently trying to ascend the Anchor of War within the First Forge, however there was a trap, half the party now must fight Vellem the Reforged and the other half must fight Reginald Providence, Lich of Therzadun.\n    68→\n    69→The party arrived with the Craftkeeper Rebels who agreed to divert the majority of the cities defenses to a mine known to serve as a well for Drab\n    70→\n    71→---\n    72→\n    73→### Slide 6\n    74→\n    75→A Trap Sprung\n    76→\n    77→The party had a pair of nasty encounters with Reginald Providence and Vellem the Reforged.\n    78→\n    79→---\n    80→\n    81→### Slide 7\n    82→\n    83→Ghost of Maghar Past\n    84→\n    85→After being teleported within the Stone Sanctum the Fireforge Siblings and Nocturnus Wayne were met with 13 statues of the Maghar who gave their lives to open the Zenith. But they are not alone as it seems that Therzadun’s influence has taken a particular liking to Lilly of Goodbarrel who is being treated as Grumble 2.0 by the clearly insane Reginald. But the true fight is for the hearts and blessings of the Maghar. And thus a deadly game of tug of war for the fate of the Mountain Zenith commenced!\n    86→\n    87→Collecting all 13 boons seems to ascend the chosen person to the state of Paragon, however one of which grants immunity to fire/lava and allows the wielder to travel through magma, a power Daina had already used once\n    88→\n    89→---\n    90→\n    91→### Slide 8\n    92→\n    93→We Hate Beholders!\n    94→\n    95→Meanwhile outside the Stone Sanctum, it seemed that Vellem had succeeded in triggering the trap after a cheeky teleport of Bergs before the real fight really began. Despite the party’s best efforts and a massive LASIK attempt by Sprocket. Vellem proved to be a formidable foe who excelled at stalling this portion of the party from helping their friends with the Lich within.\n    96→\n    97→Sprocket seeing that this was a partially lost cause, kicked off a near full “retreat” within the Stone Sanctum, nearly getting himself killed as he bumped into Peraxis Phyrexia when teleporting blindly inside!?\n    98→\n    99→---\n   100→\n   101→### Slide 9\n   102→\n   103→Sometimes You Just Gotta Go Swimming!\n   104→\n   105→Midfight, frustrated at the maddening darkness used to steal the Maghar’s boons. Daina took a leap of faith after seeing the good that Lilly was able to do with the Lava-Swimming boon. If Lilly was able to cleanse the corruption on her back, perhaps Daina could use the network of magma tunnels to head back into the Zenith the way she had the first time. Instead she found herself at the First Forge, but she was not alone. Her father, Thorkon Fireforge and a ghostly image of Reginald were here as well. However this was just the place required to ascend the Anchor of War, and doing so drained the Caldera around the Mountain’s Zenith!?\n   106→\n   107→Plunging the Anchor of War into the lone bonfire, though the Iron Doors keeping Therzadun Chained continued to bang, Daina helped her father hold the door, until it was suddenly stilled?\n   108→\n   109→---\n   110→\n   111→### Slide 10\n   112→\n   113→RIP the OG\n   114→\n   115→Back at the Stone Sanctum, Peraxis and the Vellem-fighters waited in the wings while the whole Sanctum was covered in the darkness that only madness can conjure. His sister nowhere to be seen, Nocturnus blowing up the darkness with his last radiant light bombs, and Lilly still grappling with her corruption, Bergs relied on his recently re-deceased friend to make up for his crippling spore-shambling and closed the gap on Reginald Providence, one of the original four dwarves made from the Sacrifice of Ilmater and Lich of Therzadun. All at once the darkness was dispelled and with a frenzied swing of the Maul of the Phoenix, Reginald dissolved into but a mere tadpole?!\n   116→\n   117→With his rival slain, Peraxis took no time in collecting the tadpole and attempted to use its blood to interact with the now open Iron Doors to the Mountain’s Zenith!? This leads to a dogpile by Bergs and Nocturnus, down into the now empty Cauldera of the Vag Mahar! But not before a bright light escapes the prison… great\n   118→\n   119→\n   120→## Ideas for next session\n   121→\n   122→The plot thickens\n   123→\n   124→- Silence, aka Timmothy, is headed to the opened Zenith\n   125→\n   126→  - Champion of the Autumn Court: Josh Coolbreeze is sent to warn Varis of danger\n   127→\n   128→  - Reynaldine of the Autumn Court was put on trial by the Earth Genasi at the Court of Bones in Tiberious' and the parties stead\n   129→\n   130→  - The Harvest Zenith has been taken by Grazzt and co using mortals from Dawnpass\n   131→\n   132→  - Irethia is going to travel into the scene with Silence, both crazed\n   133→\n   134→  - This is going to Draw Fen to this place as well, hot on the heels of the failed Bear Zodiac and the Child of the Forest, cycle\n   135→\n   136→    - This will lead into Elementals Plotline revolving around the Anchor of Famine, Harvest Zenith\n   137→\n   138→- Barachiel the Righteous is the Representative of Bahamut within\n   139→\n   140→  - Him and his brothers are primarily a counter to Tiamat\n   141→\n   142→  - However with Gabriel in dire straights and Dominel the Just still within Winter's Zenith, not a lot Barachiel can do\n   143→\n   144→  - If party beat Irethia, Barachiel will use her as his Vessel, knowing that Sunlight in a Springtime Glade Knight of the\n   145→\n   146→"}]},"uuid":"dca95366-cf57-44e0-8a42-a27f8f9985b0","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:07.318Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6157fd32-ec3f-4586-84ca-a15a7bda134f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"f6d7d831-8c64-420f-a062-c291c24df731","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a1afca0d6ba2fa3bf","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WBGdXfD4qRC5hkHNhKtnF6","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: faction\n     3→name: \"Therzadun Sworn\"\n     4→aliases: []\n     5→category: planar\n     6→leader: \"Therzadun (sealed)\"\n     7→headquarters: \"Behind the Iron Doors at Mountain's Zenith\"\n     8→status: \"defeated\"\n     9→source: [\"intrigue-web.md\", \"factions-and-orgs.md\"]\n    10→---\n    11→\n    12→# Therzadun Sworn\n    13→\n    14→## Overview\n    15→\n    16→Servants of Therzadun, the god of destruction. **Separate from [[Vecna]] but entangled** — their goals sometimes align, sometimes conflict. Therzadun is sealed behind Iron Doors at the [[Mountain's Zenith]].\n    17→\n    18→## Members\n    19→\n    20→- **[[Reginald Providence]]** — Lich of Therzadun, [[forgesworn|Maghar of the Belt]]. In true form a Kenku, disguised with \"plastic surgery.\" Wore the cloak of the [[Raven Queen]]. Killed by [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] (Ch. 104-105), unleashing the Phoenix maul.\n    21→- **Grumble** — Reginald's apprentice and manservant\n    22→- **Vellem the Beholder** — Allied with Reginald against the party\n    23→- **Silence (Timothy)** — Therzadun's vessel, unmasked by moonbeam (Ch. 105)\n    24→- Refurbished Warforged\n    25→\n    26→## Goals & Motivation\n    27→\n    28→- Free Therzadun from behind the Iron Doors\n    29→- Use the [[Anchor of War]] for Therzadun's purposes (conflicting with [[Peraxis Phyrexia]]'s goals)\n    30→\n    31→## Key Events\n    32→\n    33→- **Ch. 104-105:** [[Reginald Providence]] killed. His true kenku form revealed. [[Anchor of War]] silenced. Silence/Timothy unmasked as Therzadun's vessel.\n    34→- [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]] revealed (Ch. 106) that he wanted to make the new Paragon part of a dwarven god, NOT Therzadun — showing the conflict between Vecna's and Therzadun's agents.\n    35→"}]},"uuid":"bceb5dc9-29c1-4a17-8805-d2a741572c7f","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:08.209Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f6d7d831-8c64-420f-a062-c291c24df731","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

The warforged has advantage on saving throws against being poisoned and is immune to disease. Magic can&rsquo;t put it to sleep.\n    20→\n    21→## Actions\n    22→\n    23→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Belt.&nbsp;Reginald Providence blesses the Belt of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Belt grants the wearer&nbsp;the ability to hold two sets of concentration&nbsp;while within range. By passing ownership of a spell currently being concentrated on while touching the statue. Only one Belt may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n    24→\n    25→"}]},"uuid":"62b18e58-9866-432c-8f29-ca608a155f51","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:08.567Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d2e51813-a511-4894-bdea-5a5e58f44acd","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

Brigit s Notes -The Death of Reginald Providence Out of the frying pan into the forge\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/Chapter_104_-_Brigit_s_Notes_-The_Death_of_Reginald_Providence_Out_of_the_frying_pan__into_the_forge.txt\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→TLDR\n     8→\n     9→https://docs.google.com/presentation/d/1gnEAjanLqsD721sk2t6ZGl6XneJh8rPs/edit#slide=id.p1\n    10→\n    11→https://docs.google.com/presentation/d/1WIHQPvmwqk_k78Qz-Lhk5Yk5biaGTrTr/edit#slide=id.p1\n    12→\n    13→First Chapter of ARCH 5\n    14→\n    15→Two gods have avatars awakened, we've got to get a seal to stop asmodeus from waking his avatar\n    16→\n    17→Main Goal - Prevent Guard’Daemon\n    18→\n    19→Mountain’s Zenith: Beneath Vag Mahar\n    20→\n    21→Anchor of War: Daina’s Axe (unascended)\n    22→\n    23→Heart of the Forge: Fused with Axe\n    24→\n    25→God: Therzadun (Sealed behind Iron Doors)\n    26→\n    27→Forest’s Zenith: Above Machu\n    28→\n    29→Anchor of Death: Brigit’s Locket\n    30→\n    31→Heart of the Wilde: Fused with Finkle Hollingsworth/Vecna\n    32→\n    33→God: Vecna (Avatar Awakened)\n    34→\n    35→Winter’s Zenith: Bynar / Halls of Winter\n    36→\n    37→Anchor of Pestilence: Horn of Winter?\n    38→\n    39→Heart of the Faith: Fen Darkeyes took it with him\n    40→\n    41→God: Tiamat (Avatar Awakened)\n    42→\n    43→Harvest’s Zenith: Autumn Feywilde in ruins of old Uma\n    44→\n    45→Anchor of Famine: Diadem last seen in the Halls of Reflection\n    46→\n    47→Heart of the Progenitors: In the hands of Grazz’t Demon Prince of Pleasure\n    48→\n    49→God: Asmodeus (Sealed with Grazz’t in possession of 10/13 seals, missing only 2 Angel Seals)\n    50→\n    51→We hit a trap. Half are inside and half are outside. Lich of Therzadun vs. Vellum the Beholder\n    52→\n    53→There's a circle of 13 mind flayers doing some kind of ritual\n    54→\n    55→13 zodiac, IMPT\n    56→\n    57→Third times the charm, lets free the capital\n    58→\n    59→Military strategy - we split up, and now it's going to be very hard to get or give help\n    60→\n    61→We avoided a fight by \"pledging\" fealty to the Wayne family. There are three drow who can kick off darkness night prophesy\n    62→\n    63→We ran into Pyraxis Phyrexia, got a warning about Vellem.\n    64→\n    65→If both eyes look upon you, you're transported to join Reginald\n    66→\n    67→We split the party! AHHH\n    68→\n    69→Daina, bergs, nocturnous, reginalad providence, mysterious henchman\n    70→\n    71→Varis, Sprocket, Brigit, HAL, and Vellem\n    72→\n    73→FIGHT FOR YOUR LIVES\n    74→\n    75→Bergs is teleported as one eye closes. Vellem laughs crazily.\n    76→\n    77→One axe, one craftkeeper, and one prince of darkness\n    78→\n    79→Below, there's a crude warforge between everyone\n    80→\n    81→A piece of the bloodstar is held by Reginald\n    82→\n    83→We made it to the first forge (yay?)\n    84→\n    85→Outside, Vellem attacks\n    86→\n    87→Sprocket takes a CON save, fails, necrotic damage\n    88→\n    89→Brigit takes a STR save, air lifts Brigit up into the air, holding her aloft.\n    90→\n    91→AND is now incapacitated\n    92→\n    93→Inside\n    94→\n    95→Reginald adjusts his beard and neck, ice that was formed on his skin cracks loudly\n    96→\n    97→Nocturnous runs around him and lights up his fists and sword, and then slashes but takes cold damage back.\n    98→\n    99→There's a lot of statues below, looking like various characters we've seen in the past - Pickle Rotgut being one of them.\n   100→\n   101→Lily's cloak is torn off, she has a pink bubblegum cancer growing out of her back.\n   102→\n   103→Nocturnous grapples Thorkin\n   104→\n   105→Outside\n   106→\n   107→Sprocket moves behind the cover of the wall, and then attacks Vellum, disintegrate WITH A PORTENT.\n   108→\n   109→Sprocket gives Vellum LASIK\n   110→\n   111→One of the eye stalks blasts off, #5, the eye stalk that shot Sprocket melts off\n   112→\n   113→Inside\n   114→\n   115→Lily's pustules pop open, and the area around them descends into darkness\n   116→\n   117→Daina looks around - there's dark green spores, coating the floor around the forge\n   118→\n   119→Reginald is directly in it, but just fine?\n   120→\n   121→Each statue represents a dwarf in the slide deck. One of them, the spear, Torbald Greyspear. Reginald takes the spear and gets its blessing.\n   122→\n   123→And reginald gets the first 13 blessings\n   124→\n   125→Lily knows that the door will open, and it's time to fight\n   126→\n   127→Reginald blames Nocturnous, and that he made him what he is today.\n   128→\n   129→SAVE wisdom saving throw, still take psychic damage\n   130→\n   131→Reginald does the same thing to Bergs, he was there, he witnessed the ascension, the birth.\n   132→\n   133→FAIL wisdom saving throw. The voices mix together and Bergs has no movement speed for the next turn\n   134→\n   135→Outside\n   136→\n   137→Brigit is incapacitated (waiting on left)\n   138→\n   139→Vellum moves a little bit\n   140→\n   141→Brigit gets hit by another ray, but DEX's out of it\n   142→\n   143→Varis moves to get out of the circle, hunter's mark, (everyone gets benefit), and attacks with the longbow, hits and hits\n   144→\n   145→Takes FAIL wisdom saving throw, feels a second Varis second guessing everything he does. The magic items are having a hard time holding concentration.\n   146→\n   147→Inside\n   148→\n   149→Something appears and then disappears into the darkness\n   150→\n   151→Bergs hears a voice in the darkness, one of the statues, 'craftmaster, we are here'. Pickle's thing was taken already.\n   152→\n   153→Everyone looks like they were when they died, Bartrim is here as well\n   154→\n   155→The spores are similar to Bergs' but different.\n   156→\n   157→Daina calls out to her father, try to get him to work with them. There are two of him? There's something happening and the doors won't stay closed\n   158→\n   159→Calls out - protect the realm\n   160→\n   161→She receives an image of Gavin Halthrum, the uncle of Sylvia, also under the effect of mind flayers.\n   162→\n   163→She receives a blessing\n   164→\n   165→Reginald mass suggests again\n   166→\n   167→Lily's fated time has come, but takes poison damage and is diseased\n   168→\n   169→Big moment of betrayal!\n   170→\n   171→Firebolt! But misses\n   172→\n   173→And then far steps away, taking the darkness with them.\n   174→\n   175→Touches a statue and get the Hannon Jack, getting the magical greaves, and can now swim and has fire immunity\n   176→\n   177→Reginald has a legendary action, and casts a spell - Crown of Stars, each pointing towards a different statue. Thirteen shards gravitate towards a statue and Bergs is reminded of Crown of Madness (Ramses, Mordred, part of the third prophesy, crowning the paragon)\n   178→\n   179→Setting up a ritual to crown a paragon or something\n   180→\n   181→Thorkin gives Daina the ability to use his axe\n   182→\n   183→Outside\n   184→\n   185→Vellum's (left) turn, Brigit falls to the ground\n   186→\n   187→Sprocket has to make a DEX save, begins to turn to stone and is restrained\n   188→\n   189→Varis also has to make a DEX save, passes and behind him a wall begins to disintegrate\n   190→\n   191→And takes some psychic damage\n   192→\n   193→Sprocket tries another save, is restrained, passes the save\n   194→\n   195→And is no longer turning to stone\n   196→\n   197→Inside\n   198→\n   199→Daina receives some poison but is okay\n   200→\n   201→Bergs sees that the area around Lily is blighted, but he is not a normal plant\n   202→\n   203→Nocturnous touches a statues, sends out a radiant light bomb\n   204→\n   205→Armor gets blown away, things are happening.\n   206→\n   207→And he teleports into the darkness\n   208→\n   209→Reginald takes another legendary action and casts mass suggestion\n   210→\n   211→Daina gets hit with a blood star, and radiant damage. Dimension Door!\n   394→\n   395→On the ethereal plane, part of her is ripped away by a dragon - draconic passenger is gone.\n   396→\n   397→Ashara Everstar/Irethia - dark mirror of Tiamat's vessel\n   398→\n   399→Sprocket magic missile's the last three leeches.\n   400→\n   401→Until next time!\n   402→\n   403→Short short term plans\n   404→\n   405→Paul revere northward, get everyone to evacuate.\n   406→\n   407→Talk to Conquis Abbey, see if we can fix the drab with the heart of the faith\n   408→\n   409→@ Tiberious is on it\n   410→\n   411→GOT THE HEART OF THE FAITH\n   412→\n   413→Sneak into the volcano, ascend the axe\n   414→\n   415→Start the chain that frees Vag'Mahar\n   416→\n   417→Short term plans\n   418→\n   419→Scry on the children, gather their souls, ascend the locket\n   420→\n   421→Ascend the axe\n   422→\n   423→Meet up with the other group, get them to get us to the first forge. {"parentUuid":"919d9f66-6217-44cb-8f29-bc55f9fde04c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a1afca0d6ba2fa3bf","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Cxe8qYitpbP5QBizkgzwto","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"character-timelines\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/character-timelines.md\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→# Mardov Campaign -- Character Timelines\n     8→\n     9→Reconstructed from player notes (Brigit/Liz chapters 49T-136), DM timeline notes (Acts 1-4), and directory/handout evidence. Each character's journey is tracked chronologically through party splits, reunions, and pivotal moments.\n    10→\n    11→> **Confidence Legend**: [HIGH] = directly from player notes or handouts. [MEDIUM] = inferred from structural evidence. [LOW] = reconstructed from context.\n    12→\n    13→---\n    14→\n    15→## BRIGIT (Wild Spirit of Fire)\n    16→\n    17→**Player**: Liz\n    18→**Class**: Druid (Circle of the Moon) / Barbarian multiclass\n    19→**Race**: Changeling/Shapeshifter\n    20→**Key Items**: Anchor of Death (locket), Stag Zodiac\n    21→**Track**: Tieflings -> Distillery -> Good Guys Guild\n    22→\n    23→### Arc Summary\n    24→\n    25→Brigit begins as a chaotic, multi-formed druid struggling with the many voices in her head. She becomes a Progenitor through a ritual in Eden with the Children of the Forest, gains custody of the Anchor of Death in her locket, and carries the Stag Zodiac. Her arc is one of identity -- learning to channel her rage and multiplicity into purpose. She is the Wild Spirit of Fire, and through her shapeshifting, serves as the party's most versatile combatant and emotional anchor. By Act 4 she has ascended the locket, lost her elemental powers during the Harvest Zenith battle, and must find her way forward without her primary coping mechanism.\n    26→\n    27→### Timeline\n    28→\n    29→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    30→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    31→| 0-2 | Joins the party; party killed and resurrected by Sabine Ra | [HIGH] Establishes the campaign's death/rebirth theme |\n    32→| 5-7 | Hall of the Raven; early adventures | [MEDIUM] Party cohesion building |\n    33→| 13 | Liberation of Argos | [MEDIUM] First major quest completion |\n    34→| 30-37 | Underdark descent | [MEDIUM] Party faces darkness together |\n    35→| 42 | Campaign splits -- Brigit goes with Tieflings track | [MEDIUM] Party division begins |\n    36→| 49T | Cloud City heist: summons 8 boars to rescue Randall from casino, discovers fiends beneath The Roost, Fen is charmed by Minnie the succubus | [HIGH] First Cloud City arc; Brigit shows tactical creativity |\n    37→| 64T | Harvest Festival preparations: brews beer and wine with the party (Harvest Day IPA, Return of Summer Marigold Wine), creates bottle labels | [HIGH] Distillery business origins |\n    38→| 64T | Discovers drab with sun tattoo, takes pendant | [HIGH] Investigating the drab plague |\n    39→| 69T | Battles the 6-fingered Necromancer (Ned'Zailok) at the Westrun scarecrow farm, fights to burn effigies and save zodiac spirits | [HIGH] First direct confrontation with major villain |\n    40→| 69T | Dies in battle, revived by Varis' lingering Healing Spirit | [HIGH] First death (not counting Ch 2) |\n    41→| 69T | Whisked to Eden after revival | [HIGH] First contact with progenitor realm |\n    42→| 72T-76T | Alabaster Seat tournament: teams with Anemone, fights beholders, navigates political intrigue, rescues Ruth and Sheila from Mage's Prison | [HIGH] Distillery political arc |\n    43→| 77T | Trial of Sprocket: testifies in court, casts Greater Restoration on Lilith to break spellbook curse | [HIGH] Proves loyalty to party |\n    44→| 78T | Beholder trial: teams with Anemone and Olivia, Anemone destroys the beholder in 2 rounds | [HIGH] Combat prowess of allies |\n    45→| 79T | Feywilde journey: attends Summer Court tea party, votes in the House of Blood | [HIGH] Feywilde politics |\n    46→| 82T | Becomes a Progenitor through ritual in Eden with Children crystalline heart | [HIGH] End of Act 3 split |\n    52→| 87T | Performs Ritual of Reincarnation for Freld and 6 others; Daina receives Heart of the Mountain | [HIGH] Progenitor powers in action |\n
