Critical intelligence |\n    79→\n    80→### Turning Points\n    81→\n    82→1. **Death and Revival (Ch 69T)**: First real death, revived by party magic. Establishes mortality stakes.\n    83→2. **Becoming a Progenitor (Ch 82T)**: Transforms from adventurer to something more -- connected to the land itself.\n    84→3. **The Rage Speech (Ch 127)**: \"Do you have any idea how many voices exist in my head at any given time?\" -- defines her identity.\n    85→4. **Power Loss (Ch 135-136)**: Elemental magic stripped at Harvest Zenith. The silence in her head is deafening.\n    86→\n    87→### Status (as of Ch 136)\n    88→\n    89→- Alive, powers stripped (no elemental/vegan magic)\n    90→- Anchor of Death ascended but forest zenith situation unclear\n    91→- Locket pulsating abnormally (acrid green vs usual emerald)\n    92→- Must find Forest Zenith and confront zombie Finkle + demi-lich phylactery\n    93→- Needs her powers turned back on\n    94→\n    95→---\n    96→\n    97→## SPROCKET OVERSPARK\n    98→\n    99→**Player**: (unknown)\n   100→**Class**: Wizard (School of Divination)\n   101→**Race**: Gnome\n   102→**Key Items**: Third Eye (ethereal sight), Portal Gun, Robe of the Archmagi, Mordred's cursed spellbook\n   103→**Track**: Tieflings -> Distillery -> Good Guys Guild\n   104→\n   105→### Arc Summary\n   106→\n   107→Sprocket is the party's divination wizard who becomes embroiled in a cosmic chess match. Cursed by Mordred's spellbook, nearly framed as a necromancer, he rises to become the Alabaster Seat (the conduit of all arcane magic on Mardov). His third eye gives him unique sight into the ethereal plane, and his crystallization is slowly killing him. He becomes the Knight of Spring/Flower Field, and his journey is about bearing impossible burdens with humor and stubborn gnomish resolve. \"The Gnome Yoinks\" is his signature move.\n   108→\n   109→### Timeline\n   110→\n   111→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n   112→|---------|-------|-------------|\n   113→| 0-41 | United party adventures; establishes divination specialty | [MEDIUM] Foundation |\n   114→| 49T | Cloud City: identifies items, spends 300 electrum from Zephyron, investigates the Roost | [HIGH] Cloud City exploration |\n   115→| 64T | Runs brewing operations (\"Sprocket runs the shots\"), fails to recognize Randall's song about him | [HIGH] Character moment |\n   116→| 69T | Fights Necromancer: Disintegrates Volstead (Skeletor) with portent roll, saves party from total defeat | [HIGH] Signature disintegrate + portent combo established |\n   117→| 72T-76T | Alabaster tournament: cursed by Mordred's spellbook, framed as necromancer by Leon, defended by Randall in trial | [HIGH] Major political/personal crisis |\n   118→| 76T | Trial: spellbook revealed as Mordred's phylactery-like artifact, Leon exposed as true necromancer | [HIGH] Vindication, but new danger |\n   119→| 77T | Leon summons 12 Morkoths in courtroom, party fights through, Leon is killed by Fen | [HIGH] Trial conclusion |\n   120→| 78T | Beholder trial: teams with Fen and Oswald, uses Disintegrate+Portent then Phantasmal Killer to defeat beholder | [HIGH] Classic Sprocket tactics |\n   121→| 79T | Feywilde: revealed as emissary of Winter through rabbit zodiac connection, gives one drop of blood in secret vote | [HIGH] Cosmic political role |\n   122→| 82T | Final trials: dominates Ashara with portent, throws match to help Anemone become Alabaster Seat | [HIGH] Selfless act -- lets Anemone win |\n   123→| 82T | Anemone modifies Sprocket's memory so he doesn't remember choosing to lose | [HIGH] Bittersweet victory |\n   124→| 83T | Becomes Platinum Seat of Divination; explores his new office (globe map of Mardov) | [HIGH] New role |\n   125→| 84T | Sends messages to Fen and Evan; discovers sending stone spy device in tower | [HIGH] Intelligence operations |\n   126→| 85T | Controls weather to aid Battle of the Distillery; uses Earthquake scroll | [HIGH] Battlefield control |\n   127→| 86 | Reunion: Disintegrates Dallor with portent, fires tower weapons | [HIGH] End of Act 3 |\n   128→| 88-89 | Investigates tower infiltration, discovers teleportation circle in blood beneath tiles | [HIGH] Security breach |\n   129→| 90 | Falls under dream spell via Mlemph (Kuo-Toa); vision of teleportation circle's purpose | [HIGH] Prophetic vision |\n   130→| 90 | Dreams of Valley of Anchors destruction: beam of blood-red light, 6-fingered hooded figure, Gar'daemon | [HIGH] Foresight of endgame |\n   131→| 100 | Shadowfell; time dilation confusion | [HIGH] Planar adventure |\n   132→| 107 | Becomes Alabaster Seat officially (Ch 107/111 confirmed) | [HIGH] Full power assumption |\n   133→| 112 | Crystallization worsening; portal experiments; \"Rube GoldBERGS\" | [HIGH] Physical deterioration |\n   134→| 125 | Becomes Knight of Spring (replacing Tezka who lost magic) | [HIGH] Seasonal court role |\n   135→| 127-128 | Conquis Abbey: maintains anti-magic field throughout battle, writes \"Narcissa and Octavious are Frauds\" in chalk at intersection of all three circles | [HIGH] Tactical genius |\n   136→| 128 | Nearly killed by Octavious; Raven Queen brings him back personally: \"you have wonderful friends\" | [HIGH] Direct divine intervention |\n   137→| 128 | Third eye visible to everyone, crying purple tears, \"The Gnome Yoinks\" -- rips cloak/wings off Octavious with Bigsby's Hand | [HIGH] Signature moment |\n   138→| 130 | Cloud City: creates minor illusion of Keelick's voice to lure guards, finds 10th Wind in ethereal plane | [HIGH] Reconnaissance |\n   139→| 131 | Holds the shot with Varis on Nautilus railgun controls | [HIGH] Teamwork |\n   140→| 132 | Stone Table: casts Hold Monster on Gabriel, counterspells Grat'z multiple times, takes spring brazier position | [HIGH] Critical role in ritual |\n   141→| 133 | Harvest: walks in counterclockwise circles to reverse time effects, apologizes for gambling with Bergs' life | [HIGH] Self-awareness moment |\n   142→| 135 | Divinity: Snowket casts lightning at Asmodeus; Sprocket blinds, shoots portal gun, counterspells Tiamat's meteor storm | [HIGH] Maximum wizard output |\n   143→| 135-136 | Sees three spectres leave Asmodeus: one stays (shade), one goes up, one enters Nocturnus | [HIGH] Ethereal sight revelation |\n   144→\n   145→### Turning Points\n   146→\n   147→1. **The Framing (Ch 72T-76T)**: Mordred's spellbook curse nearly destroys his reputation and life.\n   148→2. **Letting Anemone Win (Ch 82T)**: Selfless sacrifice of the Alabaster Seat.\n   149→3. **The Gnome Yoinks (Ch 128)**: Signature moment of power and defiance.\n   150→4. **Counterspelling Tiamat (Ch 135)**: Counters meteor storm, saves the entire party.\n   151→\n   152→### Status (as of Ch 136)\n   153→\n   154→- Alive, crystallization ongoing\n   155→- Alabaster Seat / Knight of Spring\n   156→- Third eye attuned to ethereal plane\n   157→- Portal gun (stats still pending from DM)\n   158→- Powers likely affected by zenith magic changes\n   159→\n   160→---\n   161→\n   162→## VARIS\n   163→\n   164→**Player**: (unknown)\n   165→**Class**: Ranger (with portal/planar abilities via crystalline heart)\n   166→**Race**: Elf (Eladrin connection)\n   167→**Key Items**: Curator's Plea (bow), Horn (weapon), Ring of Air Elemental\n   168→**Track**: Tieflings -> Distillery -> Good Guys Guild\n   169→\n   170→### Arc Summary\n   171→\n   172→Varis is the party's ranger and moral compass -- a survivor of Vodram's fall who carries deep guilt about those he couldn't save. His crystalline heart grants him portal abilities, and his connection to the Autumn Court through Reynardine defines his political role. He wields the Curator's Plea and the Horn, channeling the legacy of fallen civilizations. His arc is about duty, the weight of command, and finding purpose after loss. By Act 4, he becomes Knight of Autumn and bears an almost unbearable burden of responsibility.\n   173→\n   174→### Timeline\n   175→\n   176→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n   177→|---------|-------|-------------|\n   178→| 0-41 | United party; survivor of Vodram | [MEDIUM] Foundation |\n   179→| 49T | Cloud City: goes invisible to infiltrate, encounters various NPCs at the tavern (Sam, Matthew Windsmith, Itori) | [HIGH] Diplomatic/intelligence role |\n   180→| 64T | Taste-tests brewing but fails (too drunk), meets beige ninja (air genasi) | [HIGH] Character moment |\n   181→| 69T | Fights Necromancer: 77-damage critical with lightning bowstring, goes down twice from Finger of Death | [HIGH] Nearly dies fighting Ned'Zailok |\n   182→| 69T | Vision: sees himself in a tower with the Necromancer by a fire; spirit of stag enters | [HIGH] Mystical connection to antagonist |\n   183→| 72T-76T | Tournament: enters Mage's Prison, nearly killed by Morkoths, rescued by Randall | [HIGH] Vulnerable moment |\n   184→| 78T | Beholder trial: bow destroyed by Death Ray, fights with sword and chain lightning | [HIGH] Adapts to loss |\n   185→| 79T | Feywilde: recognized as autumn emissary, sacrifices blood in the vote, feels duty hit \"like a ton of bricks\" | [HIGH] Seasonal court identity |\n   186→| 82T | Votes in 6th trial, teaches funeral customs | [MEDIUM] Cultural role |\n   187→| 83T | Buys 2 superior, 2 greater, 12 regular healing potions; sets up refugee operations at keep | [HIGH] Logistics |\n   188→| 85T | Arrives at Distillery via Kuo-Toa boat (sent by Reynardine), praised as hero on arrival | [HIGH] Homecoming |\n   189→| 86 | Reunion: Guardian of Nature form, teaches funeral customs for fallen, builds memorial | [HIGH] Leadership |\n   190→| 87T | Organizes keep operations, discovers portals in wells and at Sprocket's tower | [HIGH] Security operations |\n   191→| 88-89 | Takes stock of damage, mentors Iris (promoted to Countess), rebuilds keep | [HIGH] Commander role |\n   192→| 90 | Uses zombies as necromantic detectors, discovers portal to autumn feywilde | [HIGH] Tactical innovation |\n   193→| 100-103 | Shadowfell and reunion adventures | [MEDIUM] Continuing journey |\n   194→| 116 | Convergence and Betrayal | [HIGH] Major plot event |\n   195→| 125 | Retrieves final Asmodeus seal | [HIGH] Endgame quest completion |\n   196→| 127-128 | Conquis Abbey: phases out of world to avoid sorcerers, channels Horn and Curator's Plea, chains lightning, kills sorcerers with force shots through throats | [HIGH] Peak combat performance |\n   197→| 128 | \"In the back: I have witnessed the fall of Vodram... through me their legacy endures\" -- hears Curator's Plea speak | [HIGH] Legacy connection deepens |\n   198→| 128 | Contacted by Gabriel/Reynardine during Oblivion experience: \"I can't believe you're here. You came to save me.\" | [HIGH] Spiritual connection |\n   199→| 130 | Cloud City: follows Keelick to protect him, casts Protection from Evil and Good on the king | [HIGH] Protective instincts |\n   200→| 130 | Takes guardian of nature form, erupts feral scream \"go home and protect your families,\" uses windwall to disperse mob | [HIGH] Leadership under pressure |"}]},"uuid":"139675fa-53f0-4d35-9ac5-ec7782ee2ff0","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:15.627Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"919d9f66-6217-44cb-8f29-bc55f9fde04c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"e4712861-d1bd-489a-8afa-87d3d97762de","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a1afca0d6ba2fa3bf","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Y4vu14GtqFxBonJYU52CPx","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: faction\n     3→name: \"Forgesworn\"\n     4→aliases: [\"Mag'har\", \"The Twelve Maghar\"]\n     5→category: organization\n     6→leader: \"Historically the 12 Maghar\"\n     7→headquarters: \"[[Vag'Mahar]] (formerly), scattered\"\n     8→status: \"near-extinct\"\n     9→source: [\"intrigue-web.md\", \"Handouts/Thorkon's Note inside Daina's Axe.txt\", \"factions-and-orgs.md\"]\n    10→---\n    11→\n    12→# The Forgesworn (Mag'har)\n    13→\n    14→## Overview\n    15→\n    16→An ancient dwarven order of master weaponsmiths. Each of the 12 Maghar specializes in creating a different type of fireforged item. Their craft carries the heaviest cost in all of [[Mardov]]:\n    17→\n    18→> **A bit of yerself is imparted in each of yer creations.** There is nothing worth more than yer soul.\n    19→> — [[Thorkon Fireforge]]\n    20→\n    21→The [[forgesworn]] were hunted to near-extinction by [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] after [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]]'s betrayal was exposed.\n    22→\n    23→## The 12 Maghar\n    24→\n    25→| Title | Specialty | Known Holder | Status |\n    26→|-------|-----------|-------------|--------|\n    27→| Maghar of the Axe | Axes | [[Thorkon Fireforge]] | Captured by [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] |\n    28→| Maghar of the Hammer | Hammers | [[Hlin Blunderbeat]] | Deceased (childbirth) |\n    29→| Maghar of the Breastplate | Armor | [[Bartrim Hadrin]] | Active (exiled, leads Bartrim's Brezerkers) |\n    30→| Maghar of the Gear | Mechanisms | [[Decan Smithrunner]] | Active (runs the Dragon's Carapace) |\n    31→| Maghar of the Sword | Swords | Evelyn \"SteelHardt\" Hadrin | Deceased (killed by [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]]) |\n    32→| Maghar of the Belt | Belts | [[Reginald Providence]] | Deceased (Therzadun Sworn, killed Ch. 104-105) |\n    33→| (6 others) | Unknown | Unknown | Unknown |\n    34→\n    35→## Goals & Motivation\n    36→\n    37→- **Survival** — avoid extinction at [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]]'s hands\n    38→- **Freedom** — end the forced creation of weapons for kings\n    39→- **The Warforged Secret** — prevent the creation of more Warforged, which consume dwarven souls\n    40→\n    41→## Members & Allies\n    42→\n    43→### The Guilded Rite Rebellion\n    44→A rebellion protecting surviving [[forgesworn]]:\n    45→- [[Decan Smithrunner]] — Rebellion leader, [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]]'s trusted contact\n    46→- [[Dorrin Hammer]] — Bastard son of Rowena Bronzebeard, the only one who can build/control Warforged\n    47→\n    48→### Bartrim's Brezerkers (Mercenary Company)\n    49→- [[Bartrim Hadrin]] — Exiled Maghar, \"The Mole,\" member of the disbanded Platinum Band\n    50→- Dierdra Crestfallen — Cleric of [[Bahamut]]\n    51→- Dune — Earth Genasi Prince Seeker\n    52→- Sylvia Hadrin — Bartrim's daughter, takes up her father's sword\n    53→- Yousef Redvine — Arcaneum Collector\n    54→\n    55→### Heirs\n    56→- [[Daina Fireforge]] — Daughter of [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]], heiress to the [[First Forge]]\n    57→- [[Bergs Fireforge]] — Twin soul with [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]], also connected to the Fireforge lineage\n    58→\n    59→## Enemies\n    60→\n    61→- [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] — Betrayed [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]], persecuted the [[forgesworn]], became King of [[Vag'Mahar]]\n    62→- [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] — Captured [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]], plans Ceremorphosis\n    63→- The three Thanes of [[Vag'Mahar]] — controlled by a single Mind Flayer\n    64→\n    65→## History\n    66→\n    67→1. [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]] serves as royal weaponsmith for [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]]\n    68→2. [[Thorson Bronzebeard II|Bronzebeard]] wants to create more Warforged\n    69→3. [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]] discovers Warforged consume dwarven souls\n    70→4. [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] convinces [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]] to betray the king\n    71→5. [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]] creates a cursed soul-forged dagger as a \"gift\"\n    72→6. [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] betrays [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]], hunts all [[forgesworn]]\n    73→7. Most escape; [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]] flees with pregnant wife [[Hlin Blunderbeat|Hlin]]\n    74→8. [[Hlin Blunderbeat|Hlin]] dies giving birth to [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]] and an older brother\n    75→9. [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]] hides as \"Tor Ironforge\" in the [[Frozen Forest]]\n    76→10. Eventually captured by [[Peraxis Phyrexia]]\n    77→"}]},"uuid":"dce88538-1b28-4c95-84d1-2cff71377ed4","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:16.410Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e4712861-d1bd-489a-8afa-87d3d97762de","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"3bf4f733-812f-4816-b17b-7aca3e996b05","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a1afca0d6ba2fa3bf","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/check-discord-queue.sh\""},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01VPR7Mt4e3aDMJTwsbSxRo1","toolUseID":"toolu_01VPR7Mt4e3aDMJTwsbSxRo1","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:17.123Z","uuid":"3527ef06-1994-4e54-8228-ee3eb979439d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}
{"parentUuid":"3527ef06-1994-4e54-8228-ee3eb979439d","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a1afca0d6ba2fa3bf","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01VPR7Mt4e3aDMJTwsbSxRo1","toolUseID":"toolu_01VPR7Mt4e3aDMJTwsbSxRo1","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:17.123Z","uuid":"5276d16c-db03-4778-b51e-a108914b0dfb","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}
{"parentUuid":"3bf4f733-812f-4816-b17b-7aca3e996b05","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a1afca0d6ba2fa3bf","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VPR7Mt4e3aDMJTwsbSxRo1","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"story-arc-dm-notes\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/story-arc-dm-notes.md\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→# Mardov Story Arc — DM's Meta-Narrative and Plans\n     8→\n     9→*Extracted from DM's personal notes: story arch ideas.txt, dnd overarching story.txt, conflicts.txt, Prophesies and Zeniths.docx, Zodiacs and Player Symbolism.txt, ideas.txt, story idea bites.txt*\n    10→\n    11→---\n    12→\n    13→## The Core Thesis\n    14→\n    15→The entire campaign is built on the concept of **twin souls** — a cosmic mechanic originating from the gods Vecna and Sabine, who were the original twin souls. Vecna's jealousy of Sabine's additional powers (she rules life AND death, making her also the goddess of fate) drives the entire meta-narrative. Every major conflict is a fractal echo of this divine sibling rivalry.\n    16→\n    17→---\n    18→\n    19→## Mordred's Arc (The Primary Antagonist)\n    20→\n    21→### Phase 1: The Lover\n    22→Mordred originally wanted to resurrect Sabine, his lost love. This is the sympathetic core of his villainy.\n    23→\n    24→### Phase 2: The Collector\n    25→After Sabine's spirit was shattered, he pivoted to collecting her gifts and controlling her power. This mirrors what V-MORDRED does in the parallel dimension.\n    26→\n    27→### Phase 3: The Conspirator\n    28→- Convinced the Avatar of the Raven Queen to sacrifice her immortality\n    29→- This sacrifice created a rift between the material world and V-MARDOV (Vecna's domain)\n    30→- Gave up his own phylactery to infiltrate and destroy the Artificer's Guild\n    31→- This granted him Xerxes's body (corrupting the beings that compose Xerxes)\n    32→\n    33→### Phase 4: The Herald\n    34→With his new body, Mordred has a single goal: destroy all keystones to enable V-MORDRED's invasion. He is no longer acting for love but as an agent of the apocalypse.\n    35→\n    36→---\n    37→\n    38→## The Four-Zenith Structure\n    39→\n    40→The DM has designed the campaign as four parallel quest lines, each involving:\n    41→1. A **geographic conflict** (war, famine, pestilence, or systemic corruption)\n    42→2. A **Zenith** to cleanse (ancient nexus of power)\n    43→3. An **Anchor** to recover (weapon tied to a Horseman)\n    44→4. A **Heart** to secure (gemstone keystone)\n    45→5. **Three player characters** whose personal arcs interweave with the conflict\n    46→6. A **final battle station** where one player mans each Zenith\n    47→\n    48→### Arc 1: Harvest's Zenith (Famine) — Tygras Logath / DawnHaven\n    49→\n    50→**The Conflict:** Orc-Human war. Drought has destroyed the Harvestlands. An orc chieftain, corrupted by the same fae demon that empowered Tygras, is besieging DawnPass.\n    51→\n    52→**The Discovery:** The resurgence of orc sorcerers matches to the day with the last time it rained — supernatural famine. The Matrim Pact (humans, gnomes, half-elves united on the Maturin Turtle) holds the key.\n    53→\n    54→**The Dungeon:** The Harvest's Zenith is inside a buried pyramid (the great ziggurat), where Tygras himself still exists as an arcane being, feeding off the Zenith's internal energies.\n    55→\n    56→**The Choice:** After destroying Tygras's echo, Nyx meets the fae demon. She inherits the Mark of Tygras (Sable's Beacon). She can either rid the world of \"demon children\" (end tieflings) or rebuild the lost empire by adding tieflings to the Matrim Pact.\n    57→\n    58→**The Revival:** To revive the Maturin guardian:\n    59→1. Remove the Anchor of Famine from the zenith top (guarded by the Harvest Effigy)\n    60→2. Return the Heart of the Progenitors to the center of Tygras Logath\n    61→3. Speak: \"On his back all vows are made, a haven for the continent of dawn paid.\"\n    62→4. The turtle skeleton beneath the bay revives — a massive turtle carrying an Atlantean city, serving as THE landing point between the material world and the Fae\n    63→\n    64→**Player Arcs:**\n    65→- **Nyx (Owl):** Discovers her patron's last patronee was Tygras. Her sister Xyn's soul was the price. Wields Heart of Progenitors for all warlock rituals.\n    66→- **Irethia (Fox):** Discovers Aasimar vs. Tiefling distinction. Mans the Harvest Zenith in final battle.\n    67→- **Tesca (Mouse):** Discovers the Diadem of Dawn IS the Anchor of Famine — one wish that permanently removes spellcasting. Needed to destroy Tygras's Fae-Machine.\n    68→\n    69→### Arc 2: Winter's Zenith (Pestilence) — Bynar\n    70→\n    71→**The Conflict:** The dragon-kin tribe (Winter's Curators) is ruined. A fae eel serpent has swallowed the Heart of the Faith. Yuan-Ti stole the Anchor of Pestilence.\n    72→\n    73→**The Discovery:** The Sunless Citadel in the Shifting Sands is a Yuan-Ti fortress and shrine to the Serpent's Chosen. There is no Winter's Zenith at the citadel — the real one is within the mountain of Bynar, guarded by the Winter Dragon (White Dragon).\n    74→\n    75→**The Irony:** Stalwart's drive for revenge leads him to the truth. There's no zenith where he expected one, but he finds the Shield of Retribution instead.\n    76→\n    77→**Player Arcs:**\n    78→- **Randal (Turtle):** Must find the pan flute and resurrect the turtle Mia. The pan flute IS the Anchor of Pestilence. Grants anti-inspiration (debuff enemies) and group inspiration.\n    79→- **Stalwart (Mongoose):** Discovers Heart of Faith was stolen by White Dragon. Shield of Retribution + Heart of Faith = channel divine magic without a deity (pure retribution).\n    80→- **Dallor (Serpent):** Determines the Sunless Citadel is a Serpent's Chosen shrine. The ascended will call the serpent to defend her. Mans Winter's Zenith in final battle.\n    81→\n    82→### Arc 3: Mountain's Zenith (War) — Vag Mahar\n    83→\n    84→**The Conflict:** Dwarven civil war. Three Thanes squabble. Three secret police forces (Blackguard, Truth's Light, Silent Flame) are all secretly controlled by a single Mind Flayer.\n    85→\n    86→**The Discovery:** The Mind Flayer wants the Anchor of War (a hammer capable of earthquake-sinking Vag Mahar) to breed better Duergar. The forgotten First Forge must be relit.\n    87→\n    88→**The Resolution:** The bastard Dorrin (connected to Bergs/Daina) is the only one who can build/control Warforged. His golem Roc proves his lineage. He must be crowned Paragon.\n    89→\n    90→**Player Arcs:**\n    91→- **Daina (Wolverine):** Forgelord's daughter, twin soul with Bergs. Wields Anchor of War (half hammer-half axe, half javelin) to harness the First Flame and transform Bergs.\n    92→- **Bergs (Wolf):** Must give up his wild/natural self. Heir to First Forge. Becomes a Warforged powered by Heart of the Forge. When combined with all four Hearts, he's the Voltron power conduit.\n    93→- **Nocturnus (Bat):** Noble nephew to the Underdark vampire queen. Must kill his aunt (who gave her soul to Tiamat via bat-worship ascension). Mans Mountain's Zenith in final war.\n    94→\n    95→### Arc 4: Forest's Zenith (Death) — Machu\n    96→\n    97→**The Conflict:** The king of Machu is trapped in madness. Mordred/Ramses is the \"madness prisoner.\" The Forest's Zenith has already been partially plundered by Mordred (he had the Heart of the Wilde and used it to awaken the forest guardian — the big boss).\n    98→\n    99→**The Discovery:** After defeating the forest guardian, the Heart of the Wilde transports the ENTIRE party to V-MARDOV — inside the Death's Zenith — where they fight Lich Ned'zailok (Keeper of Death's Anchor).\n   100→\n   101→**The Resolution:** Must use a sun-reflecting mirror to access the Zenith. Free the king from madness. Bring peace to the star-crossed three:\n   102→- Xerxes's soul: held prisoner at the bottom of the Zenith\n   103→- Ramses's soul: in Daina's bag, must be returned to the phylactery for cleansing\n   104→- Sabine: appears in the flash as both bodies merge into a demi-lich\n   105→\n   106→**Player Arcs:**\n   107→- **Sprocket (Rabbit):** Vessel for Sabine Ra's return. Gains Sabine's cloak (luck feat: 3 rerolls/long rest), can channel necromancy, wields Anchor of Death in final fight.\n   108→- **Brigitte (Giant Elk):** Collects all 12 zodiac forms. Each grants psychic connection + animal form + ability to locate the zodiac being. Zodiac Locket at bottom of Tomb of the First Curator = unlimited shapeshifts + 2 Wisdom.\n   109→- **Varris (Doe):** Linked to Lich Ned'zailok in V-MARDOV. Sits in Forest's Zenith control room during final fight.\n   110→\n   111→---\n   112→\n   113→## The Prophecy Structure\n   114→\n   115→The DM uses a **layered prophecy system** — three prophecies of increasing specificity, delivered by different sources:\n   116→\n   117→1. **Ruth's Divination** (first, broadest): Establishes the stakes — anchors falling, hearts stolen, Vecna coming, blood star heralding war. Four tarot cards drawn: tiger (wind), forge (creation), holy knight (faith), family (progenitors).\n   118→\n   119→2. **The Hags' Divination** (second, nursery rhyme): \"Double bubble toil and trouble\" — confirms the first zenith (Forest) is already compromised, pestilence is being prepared with children's souls, anchors being lifted and hearts smuggled.\n   120→\n   121→3. **Mia's Divination** (third, specific/actionable): Four verses mapping exactly to four locations with specific conditions for resolution. This is the quest roadmap.\n   122→\n   123→**Design Intent:** Each prophecy is more actionable than the last. Ruth gives the cosmic scale, the Hags give the immediate threat, Mia gives the actual quest structure.\n   124→\n   125→---\n   126→\n   127→## The Final Battle (Guard'Daemon / Armageddon)\n   128→\n   129→**Setup:** V-MORDRED's invasion. The blood star falls. All four Zeniths must be held simultaneously.\n   130→\n   131→**Battle Stations:**\n   132→- Forest's Zenith: **Varris** (control room)\n   133→- Mountain's Zenith: **Nocturnus**\n   134→- Winter's Zenith: **Dallor**\n   135→- Harvest's Zenith: **Irethia**\n   136→\n   137→**Champions (Anchor wielders):**\n   138→- Anchor of Death (sword): **Sprocket**\n   139→- Anchor of War (hammer-axe-javelin): **Daina**\n   140→- Anchor of Pestilence (pan flute): **Randal**\n   141→- Anchor of Famine (Diadem of Dawn / one wish): **Tesca**\n   142→\n   143→**Power Conduit:**\n   144→- **Bergs** as Warforged powered by Heart of the Forge, channeling all four Hearts in a Voltron-style beam convergence\n   145→\n   146→**Heart Champions:**\n   147→- Arcane (Forge): Bergs\n   148→- Radiant (Faith): Stalwart\n   149→- Natural (Wilde): Brigitte\n   150→- Fae (Progenitors): Nyx\n   151→\n   152→---\n   153→\n   154→## Unused Ideas and Story Bites\n   155→\n   156→### The Colosseum City\n   157→A city that forces inhabitants to fight their way to the center circle. Citizens can only be saved by reaching the center and winning single combat. Could be a gladiatorial side-arc or the orc chieftain's domain.\n   158→\n   159→### The Dome\n   160→A giant dome of blackness, open at the top. Party must find a MacGuffin in the dark and make their way out. A dungeon concept — could be deployed anywhere.\n   161→\n   162→### The Underground Crime Network\n   163→- NPC tells party about \"the underworld\" — it's a trap\n   164→- Railcart getaway with bombs in back carts covering tracks\n   165→- The bombs are actually breaches for an invasion\n   166→- **THE PARTY IS FRAMED FOR THE INVASION**\n   167→- This is a fully-formed heist-gone-wrong arc\n   168→\n   169→### Miscellaneous Combat Ideas\n   170→- Misty Step Rogue: Grimnak\n   171→- Badass ally dimension doors in, saves the party, dimension doors out before they come to\n   172→- Battlefield with elemental planes and affinities (environmental combat mechanics)\n   173→\n   174→### The Silver Scales of Bahamut\n   175→Two philosophical factions within Bahamut's priesthood:\n   176→- Those who judge by **Justice** (what is fair by law)\n   177→- Those who judge by **Righteousness** (what is morally correct)\n   178→- The tension: \"All that is Just is not Righteous! All that is Righteous is not Just!\"\n   179→- This could fuel internal conflict within Conquis Abbey\n   180→\n   181→---\n   182→\n   183→## DM Design Patterns\n   184→\n   185→### Thematic Mirroring\n   186→Every level of the narrative mirrors the Vecna/Sabine dynamic:\n   187→- Cosmic: Vecna vs. Sabine (twin souls, jealousy)\n   188→- Mortal: Mordred's love for Sabine → corruption\n   189→- Player: Twin soul mechanics (Daina/Bergs, Nyx/Xyn)\n   190→- Structural: Material plane vs. V-MARDOV\n   191→\n   192→### The Monkey's Paw Principle\n   193→Nothing comes free. Every power has a cost:\n   194→- Magic items have hidden downsides\n   195→- The Diadem of Dawn grants a wish but removes spellcasting forever\n   196→- Nyx gains full warlock power at her sister's cost\n   197→- Bergs must give up his natural self to become Warforged\n   198→\n   199→### Player Agency Through Choice\n   200→The DM consistently designs climactic moments as binary choices:"}]},"uuid":"b5ef8beb-508f-4c71-bc1e-c95a53231fd3","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:17.122Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3bf4f733-812f-4816-b17b-7aca3e996b05","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"debe59c3-817d-4153-a0fc-5cc8a1cfc758","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a1afca0d6ba2fa3bf","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013ac82bfVT5DeCJ3UNpTTjV","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"intrigue-web\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/intrigue-web.md\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→# The Mardov Intrigue Web\n     8→\n     9→*A comprehensive map of every conspiracy, betrayal, hidden agenda, and political thread across 136 chapters.*\n    10→\n    11→---\n    12→\n    13→## Part 1: The Conspiracy Web\n    14→\n    15→### THE COSMIC ENGINE: Vecna vs. Sabine\n    16→\n    17→The entire campaign is driven by one act of divine jealousy. Vecna and Sabine were the original **twin souls** -- both gods of death, but Sabine also ruled life and fate. Vecna's envy of that additional dominion is the cosmic engine driving every conspiracy in Mardov.\n    18→\n    19→Sabine's avatar (the Raven Queen) founded two colleges of magic: the **Circle of the Murder** (necromancy/death, entrusted to her teacher Ramses) and the **Artificer's Guild** (animancy/life, entrusted to her husband Xerxes). Ramses grew jealous and attempted to grant both himself and Sabine eternal life through lichdom. When Sabine rejected the ritual, Ramses forced it on her. Sabine starved her lich form by refusing to consume souls, crystallizing into a Demi-Lich trapped inside a phylactery for centuries.\n    20→\n    21→This created the fracture that Vecna exploits across the entire campaign.\n    22→\n    23→---\n    24→\n    25→### VECNA'S FOUR ZENITHS\n    26→\n    27→Vecna's conquest is organized through four domains of power, each with a lieutenant, a target, and a nested web of agents:\n    28→\n    29→```\n    30→VECNA (God of Evil Secrets, Animancy)\n    31→|\n    32→|-- Commands all four Zenith operations\n    33→|-- Rules V-MARDOV (parallel dimension, mirror of material plane)\n    34→|-- Goal: Destroy all keystones, enable V-MORDRED's invasion\n    35→|\n    36→+-- ZENITH OF DEATH (Forest's Zenith, Machu)\n    37→|   |-- Heart: Heart of the Wilde (Emerald)\n    38→|   |-- Anchor: Anchor of Death (obsidian sword)\n    39→|   |-- Eye: Green Eye of Vecna\n    40→|   |\n    41→|   |-- MORDRED / RAMSES (Primary Antagonist)\n    42→|   |   |-- Phase 1: Lover (wanted to resurrect Sabine)\n    43→|   |   |-- Phase 2: Collector (gathered her gifts after her spirit shattered)\n    44→|   |   |-- Phase 3: Conspirator (convinced Raven Queen avatar to sacrifice\n    45→|   |   |   immortality, creating rift to V-MARDOV; gave up phylactery to\n    46→|   |   |   infiltrate Artificer's Guild; took Xerxes's body)\n    47→|   |   |-- Phase 4: Herald (destroy all keystones for V-MORDRED's invasion)\n    48→|   |   |\n    49→|   |   |-- OPERATIONS:\n    50→|   |   |   |-- Scarlet Tunic Brigade (soul-harvesting front -- 2,000 souls)\n    51→|   |   |   |-- Possessed Finkle Hollingsworth as vessel/disguise\n    52→|   |   |   |-- Deployed Jorgen as lure to recruit adventurers\n    53→|   |   |   |-- Golden Reach (Markus/Danelle, incubus/succubus agents in Argos)\n    54→|   |   |   |-- Hag Coven (Margaret, Gertrude, Tabitha -- brew Pestilence\n    55→|   |   |   |   from corrupted children's souls)\n    56→|   |   |   |-- Mandu (corrupt Argos guard captain, controlled through bribes)\n    57→|   |   |   |-- Reva (Machu Royal Advisor, source of corruption in Machu)\n    58→|   |   |   |-- Tatiana (the Imposter of Machu)\n    59→|   |   |   |-- Jafar (Sally's uncle, hired the Dons to kidnap Sally,\n    60→|   |   |   |   placed Nathan Yanagan as spy on Thornberry Shade)\n    61→|   |   |   |-- Costa Una (traitor within Scarlet Tunic Brigade)\n    62→|   |   |\n    63→|   |   |-- AGENTS: Salina (Death Knight Captain), Lord Soth (Death Knight\n    64→|   |   |   of Vodram)\n    65→|   |\n    66→|   |-- NED'ZAILOK (Lich, Keeper of Death's Anchor in V-MARDOV)\n    67→|   |   |-- Used by Vecna for experiments\n    68→|   |   |-- Betrayed Casaval Dekun (Eladrin warrior)\n    69→|   |   |-- Guards the Death's Zenith in V-MARDOV\n    70→|   |\n    71→|   |-- FOREST GUARDIAN / FOREST TITAN\n    72→|       |-- Awakened by Mordred using the Heart of the Wilde\n    73→|       |-- Boss of Forest's Zenith\n    74→|       |-- Defeating it transports party to V-MARDOV\n    75→|\n    76→+-- ZENITH OF WAR (Mountain's Zenith, Vag Mahar / Underdark)\n    77→|   |-- Heart: Heart of the Forge (Garnet)\n    78→|   |-- Anchor: Anchor of War (hammer-axe-javelin, Daina's Axe when ascended)\n    79→|   |-- Eye: Red Eye of Vecna\n    80→|   |\n    81→|   |-- PERAXIS PHYREXIA (Illithilich -- THE Central Villain)\n    82→|   |   |-- Origin: Raised by Vecna's power, gifted a shard of the Raven Queen\n    83→|   |   |-- Has all of \"Tiny Rick's\" memories\n    84→|   |   |-- One command from Vecna: \"Assault the War's Zenith\"\n    85→|   |   |-- Corrupted the Elder Brain via Vecna's influence\n    86→|   |   |   (only Ulitharid elite escaped)\n    87→|   |   |\n    88→|   |   |-- OPERATIONS:\n    89→|   |   |   |-- Created the Spell Plague (with Alhoon Ren's research)\n    90→|   |   |   |-- Captured the Stag Zodiac (Shi-Shi-Gami) to locate\n    91→|   |   |   |   Mountain's Zenith\n    92→|   |   |   |-- Holds Thorkon Fireforge captive; plans Ceremorphosis\n    93→|   |   |   |   (mindflayer conversion) as leverage against Daina\n    94→|   |   |   |-- Traded one conversation between Thorkon and Daina for\n    95→|   |   |   |   Thorkon's compliance with Ceremorphosis\n    96→|   |   |   |-- \"Now that we know you're alive, we have a spare\" (Daina)\n    97→|   |   |   |-- Beholder relay network for psychic communication\n    98→|   |   |   |-- Homunculus Thraalm deployed as agent (killed Ch. 66)\n    99→|   |   |   |-- Contacted Daina via beholder relay -- cruel mind games\n   100→|   |   |   |-- Sought to make the new Paragon part of a dwarven {"parentUuid":"c076f675-429a-42bc-99d7-c2691c3268fd","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a1afca0d6ba2fa3bf","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SFHYXrupMSDP3ebgmpysqr","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: location\n     3→category: dungeon\n     4→name: Corpseforge\n     5→parent: \"[[underdark]]\"\n     6→aliases:\n     7→  - The Ancient Dwarven Temple\n     8→notable_npcs:\n     9→  - \"[[King Korrin Phyrexia]]\"\n    10→  - \"[[Peraxis Phyrexia]]\"\n    11→  - \"[[Theodosia Thexis]]\"\n    12→chapters:\n    13→  - Act 3\n    14→source: Places/Underdark/Corpseforge/\n    15→maps: []\n    16→tags:\n    17→  - dungeon\n    18→  - corpseforge\n    19→  - underdark\n    20→  - dwarven\n    21→  - moradin\n    22→---\n    23→\n    24→# Corpseforge\n    25→\n    26→An ancient dwarven temple in the [[underdark]] that once channeled the power of [[moradin]], god of dwarves. Now corrupted, the Corpseforge contains the ghost of [[King Korrin Phyrexia]] and serves as a site of forbidden creation rituals. Its ziggurat architecture predates modern dwarven civilization.\n    27→\n    28→## Notable Features\n    29→\n    30→- Old Monethern Ziggurat -- Ancient stepped pyramid\n    31→- Arcane Observation Station\n    32→- Sandstone Doorway to the Temple\n    33→- Pit Fight arena\n    34→- **Chamber Channeling the God of Dwarves Moradin** -- The heart of the temple\n    35→\n    36→## Chamber of Moradin\n    37→\n    38→The innermost sanctum where dwarven creation rituals were performed:\n    39→- Arcana Ghost of [[King Korrin Phyrexia]] -- Spectral king\n    40→- Dwarf Creation Ritual -- Ancient rite\n    41→- [[Flemmin Fireforge]] -- Present-day dwarf\n    42→- [[Laudgar Duergar]] -- Duergar connection\n    43→- [[Maghar Greyspear]] -- Warrior\n    44→- [[Reginald Providence]] -- Maghar of the Belt, Therzadun Sworn Lich\n    45→\n    46→## Key NPCs\n    47→\n    48→- **[[King Korrin Phyrexia]]** -- Ghost of the ancient king, addresses his people\n    49→- **[[Peraxis Phyrexia]]** -- Duke of Old Monethern (same family as the ghost king)\n    50→- **[[Theodosia Thexis]]** -- Research Assistant to the King\n    51→- **[[Ambassador Uriella Moore]]** -- Duchess of Machu, present at the Corpseforge\n    52→\n    53→## Gallery\n    54→\n    55→![[assets/maps-source/Underdark/Corpseforge/Old Monethern Ziggurat.jpg]]\n    56→![[assets/maps-source/Underdark/Corpseforge/Arcane Observation Station.jpg]]\n    57→![[assets/maps-source/Underdark/Corpseforge/King Korrin Adressing his People.jpg]]\n    58→![[assets/maps-source/Underdark/Corpseforge/Sandstone Doorway to the Temple.jpg]]\n    59→"}]},"uuid":"a85edff9-02e0-4caa-a585-0d091b2b8875","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:30.384Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c076f675-429a-42bc-99d7-c2691c3268fd","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

High level.\n    26→mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/fighting-styles.md-186-**Primary Stats**: STR > CON > WIS (melee barbarian, axe-thrower, incredible toughness)\n    27→/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/fighting-styles.md-187-\n    28→--\n    29→/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/fighting-styles.md-192-\n    30→/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/fighting-styles.md-193-### Signature Abilities\n    31→mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/fighting-styles.md:194:- **Rage**: Her defining combat state. \"Daina breaks free and rages\" (Ch100). \"Daina rages, hits Reginald, there are now 13 dwarves haunting him\" (Ch104). \"Daina is riding on Bigsby's hand and enters a rage\" (Ch105). She rages as naturally as breathing.\n    32→mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/fighting-styles.md-195-- **Reckless Attack** (implied): The volume of hits per turn -- \"starts swinging at the dragon. Hitting a lot.\" -- suggests Reckless Attack with advantage on every swing.\n    33→mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/fighting-styles.md:196:- **Ancestral Dwarves**: When she \"tinks\" the Anchor of War, Maghar dwarves appear as spectral guardians (Ch109). \"13 dwarves haunting him\" when she rages against Reginald (Ch104). This is either Ancestral Guardian barbarian or a weapon-specific feature.\n    34→mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/fighting-styles.md-197-- **Throwing the Anchor of War**: \"Throws the axe at the Beholder\" (Ch105). \"Throws the Anchor of Yeet and hits Lolth\" (Ch108). She can throw her magical greataxe at range and it returns.\n    35→mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/fighting-styles.md-198-- **Lava diving**: \"Daina dived into the lava and ascended the axe\" (Ch105). She is supernaturally tough.\n    36→--\n    37→mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/fighting-styles.md-205-- Ch101: The Anchor of War lights up when she gets angry, she throws the axe at the dao -- \"hits both times!\" Emotional state directly powers weapon.\n    38→mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/fighting-styles.md-206-- Ch102: Hacks at the magical box holding Tiberius and Nocturnus -- \"cracks in two swings.\" Brute force problem-solving.\n    39→mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/fighting-styles.md:207:- Ch104: Rages, hits Reginald -- \"there are now 13 dwarves haunting him.\" Ancestral spirits manifest through combat fury.\n    40→mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/fighting-styles.md-208-- Ch105: Dives into lava to ascend the Anchor of War. Then plunges it into the volcano to make it dormant. Rides Bigsby's Hand while raging. Throws the axe at a Beholder.\n    41→mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/fighting-styles.md-209-- Ch108: \"Throws the Anchor of Yeet and hits Lolth.\" She threw her axe at a GOD and named it accordingly.\n    42→--\n    43→/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/fighting-styles.md-231-\n    44→/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/fighting-styles.md-232-### Weapons & Equipment\n    45→mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/fighting-styles.md:233:- **Maul of the Phoenix** (maul): His signature weapon through mid-campaign. Made the final blow on Reginald (Ch105). Melted in his hand during a major event (Ch107).\n    46→mardov-vault/sources/lore-extraction/fighting-styles.md-234-- **PeaceForger** (maul/hammer): Replacement weapon forged when Bergs and Daina \"jumped in without looking\" and Daina released Anemone -- \"his maul turned into the PeaceForger\" (Ch108). Me? The Creator of the most powerful Spellplague in the History of Mardov. But no! Rimefang couldn’t be trusted with War, but War will be upon us soon. Then I will claim my rightful place.\n    32→\n    33→~ Peraxis Phyrexia, Vampiric Mindflayer Lich of Vecna\n    34→\n    35→---\n    36→\n    37→### Slide 3\n    38→\n    39→Last We Saw Our Heroes…\n    40→\n    41→---\n    42→\n    43→### Slide 4\n    44→\n    45→Goals Cheat Sheet\n    46→\n    47→Main Goal - Prevent Guard’Daemon\n    48→\n    49→Mountain’s Zenith: Beneath Vag Mahar\u000bAnchor of War: Daina’s Axe (Ascended)\u000bHeart of the Forge: Fused with Axe\u000bGod: Therzadun (Sealed behind Iron Doors)\n    50→\n    51→Forest’s Zenith: Above Machu\u000bAnchor of Death: Brigit’s Locket\u000bHeart of the Wilde: Fused with Finkle Hollingsworth/Vecna\u000bGod: Vecna (Avatar Awakened)\n    52→\n    53→Winter’s Zenith: Bynar / Halls of Winter\u000bAnchor of Pestilence: Horn of Winter?\u000bHeart of the Faith: Given to Brigit by Fen Darkeyes\u000bGod: Tiamat (Avatar Awakened)\n    54→\n    55→Harvest’s Zenith: Autumn Feywilde in ruins of old Uma\u000bAnchor of Famine: Diadem last seen in the Halls of Reflection\u000bHeart of the Progenitors: In the hands of Grazz’t Demon Prince of Pleasure\u000bGod: Asmodeus (Sealed with Grazz’t in possession of 10/13 seals, missing only 2 Angel Seals)\n    56→\n    57→---\n    58→\n    59→### Slide 5\n    60→\n    61→Champion of Dusk\n    62→\n    63→We cut to Joshua Coolbreeze, Champion of Dusk, in the middle of an assault on the ruins of old Uma at the Towers of Twilight. However, he and the Autumn Court were betrayed by their newest recruits Silence and his mother Umbara Skuttle, who used an air elemental attack to ambush Sunlight in a Springtime Glade, killing the Tabaxi turned Tiefling. Using some ancient Right of the Stone Table, Umbara was able to cripple Reynaldine, forcing Josh to find his Commanding Officer, Varis Laidon, Knight of the Dusken Wood!\n    64→\n    65→Reminders:\n    66→\n    67→Reynaldine’s cloak lost its leaves\n    68→\n    69→the Stone Table is where the Diadem of the Dawn was used to bring peace to Autumn and Spring\n    70→\n    71→Silence is also known by Timmothy, Slaad Egg infected by Ceremorphosis with a Star Spawn Mask\n    72→\n    73→---\n    74→\n    75→### Slide 6\n    76→\n    77→Caldera Beneath The Vag Mahar\n    78→\n    79→Nocturnus Wayne, Bergs and Peraxis Phyrexia all fall from the Stone Sanctum through the now drained Caldera of the Vag Mahar. Despite being enemies, Peraxis saves our heroes and leaves them with a warning, “before the day is out, a new paragon will be crowned” but of which god? {"parentUuid":"145eab1f-fa75-431f-9ce4-2f8626050084","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a1afca0d6ba2fa3bf","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01S86zawoQcrFyfC5z6x2crA","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"Chapter 106 - Brigit s Notes - Declaration of War\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/Chapter_106_-_Brigit_s_Notes_-_Declaration_of_War.txt\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→DM lead in: https://docs.google.com/presentation/d/1p4tJKzB30-nIFfi7wQGxQ2-gDTZbGz_A/edit#slide=id.p1\n     8→\n     9→TLDR:\n    10→\n    11→Reynaldine was crippled, his cloak fell\n    12→\n    13→Peraxis wanted to save us, to make the new paragon part of dwarven god, not therzadune\n    14→\n    15→The bear zodiac and fen and josh ran through the underground to the zodiac.\n    16→\n    17→Note: each zenith has a heart, anchor, domain and angel.\n    18→\n    19→Angel of the war zenith, heart of the forge, domain of war, angel of Barachiel the Righteous.\n    20→\n    21→AND each angel gets the seal of Asmodeus.\n    22→\n    23→Silence was also known as timmothy?\n    24→\n    25→Note: with Fenrir's Bargain, Bergs left the Horn of Winter in the snow. And we might have to go get it.\n    26→\n    27→And the dwarven children were saved/helped in unknown ways\n    28→\n    29→Note: Miconid network is the same as St. Ashbury's network and is the same as Berg's network\n    30→\n    31→Lots of craziness, we went to Josh's rescue, Brigit got the heart of the faith and Cycle, Fen went into the zenith\n    32→\n    33→Josh shined a moonbeam on Silence, revealing him to be Therzadune's vessel.\n    34→\n    35→Umbara Skuttle grabbed Ernie, but Brigit/Air Elemental grabbed her before she could run away\n    36→\n    37→Note: a tadpole got into Irethia\n    38→\n    39→Note: Homunculus is a portion of the Wizard's soul, directly\n    40→\n    41→So what's next?\n    42→\n    43→What should we do about Umbara?\n    44→\n    45→And let's quickly get into the zenith.\n    46→\n    47→After battle stuff\n    48→\n    49→Sprocket looks at Reginald Providence, and weirdly appears plastic\n    50→\n    51→Finds a large iron key. Like a ceremonial key-to-the-city thing.\n    52→\n    53→It's a cast iron, runework key, runes in dwarvish - Whoever so holds this key, may open the vault of the Vag'Mahar and plunder what's inside.\n    54→\n    55→His blood isn't congealing, it's weird, and he has a very strange body in general.\n    56→\n    57→Therzadune appears to be corrupting this clone, and plastic surgery addict, bonesaw.\n    58→\n    59→His cloak is a ravens head, the symbol of the raven queen.\n    60→"}]},"uuid":"43967c8f-e339-4a53-a6c6-725ea6411d20","timestamp":"2026-03-19T06:00:09.297Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"145eab1f-fa75-431f-9ce4-2f8626050084","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

   55→His blood isn't congealing, it's weird, and he has a very strange body in general.\n    56→\n    57→Therzadune appears to be corrupting this clone, and plastic surgery addict, bonesaw.\n    58→\n    59→His cloak is a ravens head, the symbol of the raven queen.\n    60→\n    61→Clothes of Reginald\n    62→\n    63→Once removing the cloak, his body turns into a kenku.\n    64→\n    65→Righteousness\n    66→\n    67→Sprocket identifies the cloak, it’s a cloak of raven-kind\n    68→\n    69→Staff - tesla coil on top, it appears to give resistance to lightning damage, and the network, something shows up onto the network and then both drop off?\n    74→\n    75→Josh notices that the kenku voice had the same voice he said in the mind flayer pit\n    76→\n    77→Daina, Brigit and Varis attempt to separate Umbara from Ernie\n    78→\n    79→Brigit gives Ernie a once over, gives the statue some mending, and a blue soul line appears\n    80→\n    81→Brigit takes one of Birt's feathers and runs greater restoration to revive Ernie.\n    82→\n    83→Birt the Ernie is back!\n    84→\n    85→Birt hangs onto Brigit's antlers that aren't actually there\n    86→\n    87→Let's leave Umbara here, with Nocturnous\n    88→\n    89→Daina checks in with the warforged, the conservatory warforged are deactivated, she'll have to get to the control room within the zenith\n    90→\n    91→Nocturnous is going to stay here, protect people from entering and watch Umbara to make sure she doesn't do anything bad.\n    92→\n    93→Bergs informs Theodosia of the plan, stop the dwarves from going this way.\n    94→\n    95→She sends back a jump scare, a drab dwarf.\n    96→\n    97→INTO THE ZENITH\n    98→\n    99→First room, there's a ruby red crystal, pulsating like a beating heart.\n   100→\n   101→Down looks like there's some fighting, a creature pushed through, leaving the rubble behind, probably bear shaped, dunno.\n   102→\n   103→Cycle said they were made to become a zodiac, not Cloyma's, but one individual zodiac.\n   104→\n   105→Daina touches the crystal, the staircase settles, the crystal opens like petals and reveals a spiral staircase.\n   106→\n   107→THE TRUE ENTRANCE.\n   108→\n   109→The bridge\n   110→\n   111→Across the drawbridge is HAL\n   112→\n   113→Brigit checks, ITS NOT HIM. His eyes are weird, but it is his body.\n   114→\n   115→And he's not on Daina's warforged network\n   116→\n   117→They have been deactivated?\n   118→\n   119→Daina checks the sturdiness of the bridge - there's an explosive device on it.\n   120→\n   121→Brigit turns into a giant eagle and takes Daina, drops her off behind HAL\n   122→\n   123→Brigit goes back for Bergs\n   124→\n   125→HAL says his the rightful owner of Daina's axe, and tries to grab it.\n   126→\n   127→A piece of Therzadune, another clone or avatar or something?\n   128→\n   129→Varis tries to banish, but it isn't able to take hold.\n   130→\n   131→HAL's ruby eyes burn red for a moment and says HELP\n   132→\n   133→ROLL FOR INITATIVE\n   134→"}]},"uuid":"d030aba7-ed32-48fc-841f-3010db11c715","timestamp":"2026-03-19T06:00:14.805Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6832ded7-1f80-46b6-8bea-136026f0f2b4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"22271182-9128-4f98-a7c6-d0ff03e1caa2","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a1afca0d6ba2fa3bf","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_012xKjESAe8EBnWugTYaUiG9","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I now have enough data to construct the complete timeline. Here is the result:\n\n---\n\n# Reginald Providence -- Complete Timeline\n\n## Identity\n\nReginald Providence is the **Maghar of the Belt** -- one of the thirteen Forgesworn master weaponsmiths who served the dwarven god Moradin. He held the title within the Forgesworn order and his Channel Divinity (Bless Artifact: Belt) granted the ability to hold two simultaneous concentration spells. His true form is a **kenku**, disguised through what the party calls \"plastic surgery\" -- a false dwarven appearance maintained through corrupted magic. He wore the **Cloak of the Raven Queen** (identified post-mortem as a Cloak of Raven-Kind).\n\nHis stat block: AC 17, HP 231, INT 22, Truesight 120ft. His soul is contained in a **Far Realm Parasite** (wormlike creature in his torso) rather than a traditional phylactery. 4 Legendary Resistances per day.\n\n---\n\n## Chronological Timeline\n\n### Ancient Era -- Origins\n\n**[CANON]** Reginald Providence was one of the **\"original four dwarves of Mardov\"** created from the **Sacrifice of Ilmater** (Ch. 105, DM slide: \"one of the original four dwarves made from the Sacrifice of Ilmater and Lich of Therzadun\"). This places his creation at the founding era of dwarven civilization in Mardov.\n\n**[CANON]** He became the **Maghar of the Belt**, one of the thirteen Forgesworn who bore Moradin's Coat of Arms. Each Maghar carried a transferable tattoo passed from master to apprentice. The combined effort of all 13 Maghar created the Warforged army. He had an apprentice/manservant named **Grumble**.\n\n**[CANON]** At some point, all 13 Maghar \"gave their lives to open the Mountain's Zenith\" (Ch. 105, DM slide). This is the death event that should have ended Reginald -- but he came back as a lich.\n\n### Corruption -- Fall to Therzadun\n\n**[CANON]** Reginald was originally **good**. He \"died to give Daina the Heart of the Forge\" as one of the original dwarves (Ch. 102 player notes). The player notes are explicit: \"He was good, but then Nocturnous gave him a drab fish\" (Ch. 102).\n\n**[INFERRED]** The \"drab fish\" is a parasitic creature connected to the Spell Plague / drab network -- a mindflayer-corrupted organism. Being fed a drab fish by Nocturnus (likely the Drow vampire, not the PC Nocturnus Wayne -- or possibly through some time-paradox connection) corrupted Reginald and opened him to Therzadun's influence.\n\n**[CANON]** He became the **first Lich of Therzadun** (Ch. 102: \"the first lich of Therzadun\"). His Far Realm Parasite soul mechanic replaced a traditional phylactery, and his powers became madness-themed: Psychic Whisper, Maddening Darkness, Mass Suggestion, parasitic tentacles that convert victims into gibbering mouthers.\n\n**[SPECULATIVE]** The alternate timeline referenced in Ch. 125 (Borgot's notes) -- \"In this different timeline, Bergs isn't present when Reginald was fed drab fish\" -- suggests the drab fish corruption event is a fixed point that happens across timelines, and that Bergs' presence (or absence) at that moment is a variable with campaign-level consequences.\n\n### The Corpseforge Era\n\n**[CANON]** Reginald Providence was present at the **Corpseforge**, an ancient dwarven temple in the Underdark, alongside King Korrin Phyrexia (ghost), Maghar Greyspear, Laudgar Duergar, and Flemmin Fireforge. This is where dwarf creation rituals were performed in the Chamber of Moradin.\n\n**[INFERRED]** His presence at the Corpseforge alongside Laudgar Duergar connects to the \"combined effort by Laudgar Duergar to create Warforged out of all 13 Maghar\" -- this was likely where the Warforged creation occurred, using the Maghar's souls/lives as fuel.\n\n### As Therzadun's Agent -- Off-Screen Activity (Acts 2-3)\n\n**[CANON]** By the time of the campaign's dwarven civil war arc, Reginald had established himself in the **Stone Sanctum** within Vag'Mahar (the dwarven capital). He occupied the area near the **Mountain's Zenith** beneath the city, alongside his ally **Vellem the Beholder** (a two-eyed beholder).\n\n**[CANON]** He held a piece of the **Blood Star** (Ch. 104 player notes). He possessed a **demiplane** that \"looks exactly like the bloodstar\" -- a mock-up of the Mountain's Zenith heart, filled with jars of elements and cast irons, because \"he was studying it\" (Ch. 109).\n\n**[CANON]** He treated his captive **Lilly of Goodbarrel** as \"Grumble 2.0\" -- a replacement for his dead apprentice -- indicating he was clearly insane by this point (Ch. 105 DM slides).\n\n**[INFERRED]** During the party splits (Acts 3-4, Chapters 42-103), Reginald was working to achieve one goal: collect all 13 Maghar blessings from the statues in the Stone Sanctum and crown himself (or his puppet) as the **new Paragon** -- but a Paragon of Therzadun, not Moradin. This would free Therzadun from behind the Iron Doors of the Mountain's Zenith.\n\n**[CANON]** His rival was **Peraxis Phyrexia** (Illithilich of Vecna). Peraxis was put \"in charge of War\" by Vecna but resented it -- he wanted Pestilence. Peraxis warned the party about Vellem's trap specifically to help them defeat Reginald (Ch. 104 DM slides).\n\n### Chapter 102 -- First Sighting\n\n**[CANON]** The party learns about Reginald during reconnaissance: \"There's a lich, the first lich of Therzadun. One of the original dwarves.\" They also learn his corruption origin: he was once good but was corrupted by a drab fish. A war between \"iron machines and drab\" rages in the area.\n\n### Chapter 104 -- The Death of Reginald Providence\n\n**[CANON]** The party assaults Vag'Mahar with the **Craftkeeper Rebels** to reach the First Forge and ascend the Anchor of War. They encounter **Peraxis Phyrexia** at the ruins of the Dragon's Carapace Workshop, who warns them about Vellem's trap: \"if both eyes look upon you, you will be transported to join Reginald within the Stone Sanctum.\"\n\n**[CANON]** The trap is sprung. The party splits:\n- **Inside the Stone Sanctum** (with Reginald): Daina Fireforge, Bergs Fireforge, Nocturnus Wayne, Lilly of Goodbarrel\n- **Outside** (fighting Vellem): Varis, Sprocket, Brigit, HAL\n\n**[CANON]** Inside, 13 statues of the dead Maghar stand in a circle. Reginald begins collecting their blessings -- a **tug-of-war for the fate of the Mountain's Zenith**. He casts Crown of Stars with 13 motes gravitating toward the statues, reminiscent of Crown of Madness (Third Prophecy: \"crowning the paragon\").\n\n**[CANON]** Key combat moments:\n- Reginald blames Nocturnus Wayne for making him what he is (\"he made him what he is today\")\n- Reginald psychically attacks Bergs: \"he was there, he witnessed the ascension, the birth\"\n- Reginald uses Mass Suggestion repeatedly, Maddening Darkness, and Crown of Stars\n- He holds the Blood Star and radiates ice (cold damage on contact)\n- Daina rages and \"13 dwarves haunt him\" -- the ancestral Maghar spirits turn against him\n- Daina acquires the lava-swimming boon from Lilly, dives into the magma tunnels to reach the First Forge\n\n**[CANON]** At the First Forge, Daina finds her father **Thorkon Fireforge** and a ghostly image of Reginald. She plunges the Anchor of War into the bonfire, ascending it. This drains the Caldera around the Mountain's Zenith and stills the Iron Doors holding Therzadun.\n\n**[CANON]** **The killing blow**: Bergs Fireforge, shambling with myconid corruption, misty-steps out of the magical darkness and, with Action Surge, strikes Reginald with the **Maul of the Phoenix**. \"Reginald, lich of Therzadun, original 4 dwarves of Mardov, erupts into flames, erupts into small cephalopods, like Timothy.\" His boons transfer to Bergs.\n\n**[CANON]** Simultaneously, the ghostly Reginald at the First Forge erupts into flames and vanishes. The Iron Doors still for a moment as Daina and Thorkon hold them shut.\n\n### Chapter 105 -- Immediate Aftermath\n\n**[CANON]** Peraxis Phyrexia immediately collects the tadpole (Reginald's Far Realm Parasite remains) and attempts to use its blood to interact with the now-open Iron Doors to the Mountain's Zenith. Bergs and Nocturnus tackle him, and all three fall into the drained Caldera.\n\n**[CANON]** A bright light escapes the Iron Doors' prison -- a signal of Therzadun's partial release. Peraxis warns: \"As the anchor of war is silenced, a new paragon is crowned. You fight today to find out if it's a paragon of Muradin or Therzadun.\"\n\n**[CANON]** Reginald \"never got all 13 powers\" (Ch. 105 player notes) -- he was stopped before completing his Paragon ritual.\n\n### Chapter 105 -- Silence/Timothy Unmasked\n\n**[CANON]** At the Autumn Feywilde (Harvest Zenith location), **Josh Coolbreeze** casts **Moonbeam** on Silence, revealing his true nature: \"Behind the mask is a writhing, wriggling, mass of bugs, tadpole things.\" Silence is confirmed as **Therzadun's vessel** -- \"it's one of Therzadun's vessels.\"\n\n**[CANON]** Silence holds out his hand, 13 tadpoles fly out onto 13 mind flayers, who all grow porcelain masks. This parallels Reginald's 13-mote Crown of Stars ritual.\n\n**[CANON]** Silence (Timothy) is identified as \"Slaad Egg infected by Ceremorphosis with a Star Spawn Mask\" (Ch. 106 DM slides). His creator is **Umbara Skuttle**.\n\n### Chapter 106 -- Post-Mortem Examination\n\n**[CANON]** Sprocket examines Reginald's corpse: \"weirdly appears plastic.\" His blood doesn't congeal. \"Therzadune appears to be corrupting this clone, and plastic surgery addict, bonesaw.\"\n\n**[CANON]** His cloak is a raven's head (symbol of the Raven Queen), identified as a **Cloak of Raven-Kind**. He also carried a staff (tesla coil on top, grants lightning resistance, prongs serve as a key).\n\n**[CANON]** \"Once removing the cloak, his body turns into a **kenku**.\" Josh Coolbreeze notes the kenku voice was the same voice he heard in the mind flayer pit.\n\n**[CANON]** A cast iron key is found on his body -- dwarvish runes read: \"Whoever so holds this key, may open the vault of the Vag'Mahar and plunder what's inside.\"\n\n**[CANON]** Peraxis reveals his true agenda (Ch. 106): he wanted to save the party -- \"to make the new Paragon part of dwarven god, not Therzadun.\" The Vecna-Therzadun rivalry is laid bare.\n\n### Chapter 109 -- Reginald's Demiplane Discovered\n\n**[CANON]** Behind the throne in Vag'Mahar, using a tuning fork and key, the party opens \"the demiplane that was Reginald Province's. Looks exactly like the bloodstar.\" Inside: jars of elements, cast irons -- \"a mock of [the Zenith] because he was studying it.\"\n\n---\n\n## Key Relationships\n\n| Character | Relationship |\n|-----------|-------------|\n| **Therzadun** | Master. Sealed behind the Iron Doors; Reginald served as his primary agent to free him. |\n| **Peraxis Phyrexia** | Rival. Vecna's agent, put in charge of War. Hated by Reginald; wanted the Paragon for a dwarven god, not Therzadun. |\n| **Vellem the Beholder** | Ally. Two-eyed beholder who guarded the Stone Sanctum entrance and served as Reginald's enforcer. |\n| **Grumble** | Apprentice/manservant. \"Maghar of the Belt\" apprentice. Status unknown. |\n| **Lilly of Goodbarrel** | Captive treated as \"Grumble 2.0.\" Had pink corruption growing from her back. |\n| **Silence/Timothy** | Fellow Therzadun agent. A star spawn vessel who carried the same tadpole-like corruption. |\n| **Nocturnus Wayne (Drow)** | Connected to his corruption (\"Nocturnous gave him a drab fish\" / \"he made him what he is today\"). |\n| **Bergs Fireforge** | Killer. Struck the final blow with the Maul of the Phoenix. Inherited Reginald's Maghar boons. |\n| **Daina Fireforge** | Opposed him at the First Forge; ascended the Anchor of War while he tried to reach her. |\n\n## Open Questions\n\n- **[SPECULATIVE]** How did a kenku become one of the \"original four dwarves of Mardov\"? Was he transformed, or was his dwarven appearance always a disguise? The \"plastic surgery\" and \"clone\" language suggests he may have been a kenku who stole/copied a Maghar identity, or an original dwarf whose lichdom transformed him into something else.\n\n- **[SPECULATIVE]** The exact mechanism of \"Nocturnous gave him a drab fish\" is unclear. Is this the PC Nocturnus Wayne, a time-loop event, or a different Nocturnus (the Drow prince before the party)? Reginald's accusation that Nocturnus \"made him what he is today\" (Ch. 104) supports a direct causal link.\n\n- **[SPECULATIVE]** What was Reginald doing during the party splits (Chapters 42-100)? He was likely in the Stone Sanctum, preparing the 13-Maghar ritual, maintaining the Iron Doors' seals for Therzadun, coordinating with the mind flayer occupation of Vag'Mahar, and studying the Mountain's Zenith in his demiplane lab.\n\n- **[INFERRED]** His Far Realm Parasite mechanic means he should reform in 2d4 days within 1d4 miles of death -- unless killed inside a magic circle for undead (which would turn him into an otyugh). Whether this triggered is never addressed in the text; he appears permanently dead."}],"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3166,"cache_read_input_tokens":102054,"output_tokens":3901,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3166},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CZBvpB6YHyi3A57Eu3Qe4","type":"assistant","uuid":"ed0afc31-dd14-4c14-beab-100f671611c4","timestamp":"2026-03-19T06:01:54.947Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}
